Abstract: Accurate assessment of student competencies is essential for enabling educators to identify individual needs, design targeted interventions, and evaluate the effectiveness of educational strategies. Empirical assessment procedures are typically grounded in psychometric models, such as item response theory, which relate student competence levels to performance on assessment tasks. In this paper, we advocate adopting a structural causal modelling approach to educational assessment, moving beyond probabilistic belief updating toward a framework that explicitly supports interventional and counterfactual reasoning. We propose a corresponding protocol for its construction and analyse the practical relevance of forms of reasoning that remain inaccessible to standard associative models, including the explicit modelling of interventions such as hints and the related counterfactual scenario analysis. Although our protocol requires the structural equations to be elicited from experts, the necessary information is purely logical and does not rely on probabilistic, less tenable assumptions. We illustrate the approach using data from an assessment that employs complex tasks designed to measure compulsory school student algorithmic skills.

</details>


### [267] [Parason: Revealing Subtask and Trial Parallelism in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24658)
*Zhengyang Zhang,Zijian Zhang,Jiaxuan Gao,Shusheng Xu,Yi Wu,Song Han,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Parason，一个同时利用子任务并行性和试验并行性来加速LLM推理的系统。分析表明试验并行性占可并行化推理计算的大多数（在HLE中达65.5%），且随问题难度增加而更加主导。Parason采用上下文无关语法将顺序推理轨迹转化为结构化并行轨迹，并通过并行感知的PA-GRPO训练模型，在AIME24和AIME25上实现约1.7倍的加速同时保持竞争力精度的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归解码顺序执行长推理轨迹，产生严重延迟（困难任务可达数天至数周）。先前系统主要关注子任务并行性，忽视了试验并行性——多路推测性尝试并行探索、验证和聚合竞争假设。作者希望揭示并利用这两种形式的并行性来缓解推理延迟瓶颈。

Method: 1）分析识别出两种并行性：子任务并行性和试验并行性，并发现后者占可并行化计算的大多数；2）利用上下文无关语法（CFG）将顺序推理轨迹转换为结构化并行轨迹；3）提出并行感知的分组相对策略优化（PA-GRPO），其奖励函数联合平衡精度、延迟和两种并行比率；4）推理时通过工具调用执行学习的并行结构，实现真实墙钟加速。

Result: 在AIME24和AIME25等数学推理基准上，Parason实现平均约1.7倍的加速，同时保持有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: 试验并行性是LLM推理中一种被忽视但占主导地位的并行形式，通过系统性引导和学习，可以将其转化为实际的推理加速收益，而不显著牺牲精度。

Abstract: Scaling test-time reasoning has substantially improved the problem-solving ability of large language models (LLMs), but standard autoregressive decoding still executes long reasoning traces sequentially, creating severe latency for difficult tasks (up to days and weeks). Parallel reasoning offers a natural remedy. However, prior systems primarily focus on Subtask Parallelism, where the model learns to decompose a high-level task into smaller chunks that can be solved independently. This approach overlooks another pervasive form of parallelism: Trial Parallelism, where multiple speculative attempts explore, verify, and aggregate competing hypotheses in parallel. In this paper, we introduce Parason, which reveals and learns both forms of parallelism in LLM reasoning. Our analysis identifies Trial Parallelism as the majority of parallelizable reasoning computation (65.5% in DeepSeek-V4's reasoning steps in HLE), and it becomes increasingly dominant on hard problems. Guided by this taxonomy, Parason converts sequential reasoning traces into structured parallel trajectories with a context-free grammar, then trains models with Parallelism-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (PA-GRPO), whose reward jointly balances accuracy, latency, and the two parallelism ratios. At inference time, Parason executes the learned parallel structure through tool calls, translating theoretical savings to real-world wall-clock acceleration. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks including AIME24 and AIME25 show that Parason achieves an average acceleration about 1.7$\times$ while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [268] [Lifted Model Construction under Approximate Commutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24713)
*Malte Luttermann,Jan Speller,Tanya Braun,Marcel Gehrke,Ralf Möller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ε-可交换性概念，放宽了提升推理中因子可交换性的严格要求，允许近似可交换的因子用于提升模型构建和概率推理，并证明了由此引入的近似误差的严格界限，在保证高查询精度的同时降低运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升推理算法依赖于识别可交换因子（即输出值对输入子集排列不变的函数）来构建提升表示。然而实际中从数据学习到的参数不可避免地存在偏差，即使关联对象不可区分，其对应因子也只是近似可交换而非精确可交换，这阻碍了提升推理的实用化。

Method: 引入ε-可交换性概念，作为可交换性的松弛——允许输出值在输入排列下近似不变。展示了如何利用ε-可交换性进行提升模型构建和下游概率推理，并证明了由此引入的近似误差的严格界限。

Result: 理论保证得到经验验证，实验表明在保持可比查询精度的同时实现了更低的运行时间。

Conclusion: ε-可交换性使得提升模型构建在实用场景中可行，既保证了高精度的查询结果，又大幅提升了推理效率。

Abstract: Lifted inference algorithms enable scalable probabilistic inference even for large object domains by leveraging the indistinguishability of objects in a probability distribution. An essential prerequisite for constructing a lifted representation is to identify commutative factors, i.e., functions whose output values are invariant under permutations of a subset of their input values, in a potential-based factorisation. In practice, however, parameters learned from data inevitably deviate even if associated objects are indistinguishable, causing their corresponding factors to be only approximately commutative instead of being exactly commutative. We address this problem by introducing the concept of ε-commutativity, a relaxation of commutativity where output values are only approximately invariant under permutations of input values. Specifically, we show how ε-commutativity can be exploited for lifted model construction, downstream probabilistic inference, and prove strict bounds on the induced approximation error, thereby ensuring the practical applicability of lifted model construction while maintaining highly accurate query results. These theoretical guarantees are confirmed empirically, demonstrating comparable query accuracy at lower runtime.

</details>


### [269] [RACE: Scalable Statistical Estimation of Functional Consistency in LLM Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24758)
*Runyu Wang,Bo Liu,Xiaxin Zhang,Yu Han,Jiawei Cao,Xiaoye Zhang,Zhe Zhang,Yifan Yang,Peng Ping*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RACE框架，一种基于前向传播的统计方法，用于评估Transformer神经元在整个领域上的功能一致性，相比梯度方法具有更高的领域特异性和更低的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机械可解释性方法在发现跨整个领域的稳定神经元行为方面存在挑战：基于实例的点估计方法掩盖了群体层面的变异性，而计算昂贵的方法限制了领域级的大规模分析。

Method: RACE（Residual Alignment for Consistency Estimation）是一种前向传播统计框架，通过残差对齐来估计Transformer神经元的领域级功能一致性，无需反向传播或昂贵计算。

Result: 扰动实验显示RACE比基于梯度的点估计方法具有更好的领域特异性；token分布级别的结果验证了所选神经元与目标领域之间的关联；计算开销比梯度方法低两个数量级。

Conclusion: RACE为领域级神经元功能一致性分析提供了一种高效且准确的替代方案，在保持更高领域特异性的同时大幅降低了计算成本，有望支持大规模机械可解释性分析。

Abstract: Discovering stable neuron behavior across entire domains remains a challenge in mechanistic interpretability. Existing methods often rely on instance-level point estimates or computationally expensive procedures, which either obscure population-level variability or limit scalable domain-wide analysis. We present RACE (Residual Alignment for Consistency Estimation), a forward-pass statistical framework that evaluates the domain-wide functional consistency of Transformer neurons. Perturbation experiments demonstrate that RACE achieves superior domain specificity compared to gradient-based point estimates. Meanwhile, token-distribution-level results verify the association between the selected neurons and the target domain. Furthermore, its computational overhead is two orders of magnitude lower than that of gradient-based methods.

</details>


### [270] [Evidence Blindness in Direct Corpus Interaction: Persistent Navigation with AtlasNav](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24764)
*Hongyu Guo,Zhiyu Zheng,Zhao Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AtlasNav，一个持久多视图语料库导航框架，用于大规模智能体搜索。它通过一次性将语料库组织成 Corpus Atlas（语料库图谱），使每个查询自适应导航而非重构共享结构，解决了直接语料库交互（DCI）范式下的"证据盲区"（Evidence Blindness）问题。在 BrowseComp-Plus 上达到 92.05% 的严格准确率，在线推理成本较之前动态工作区方法降低 30.21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体正从传统检索增强生成转向与外部语料库的直接交互（DCI），但在有限的交互预算下，可达证据可能无法被利用——证据无法浮出水面、支撑文档未被打开、或打开文档中决定性片段未被暴露。作者称之为"证据盲区"（Evidence Blindness）。现有 DCI 范式下，原始交互缺乏可复用的语料库组织，动态工作区方法则需为每个查询和轨迹重建查询条件化交互空间，有用结构大多在线恢复。作者认为这效率低下，应改为在可复用语料库结构上进行有限预算导航。

Method: AtlasNav 是一个持久多视图语料库导航框架。它保留直接语料库交互，但仅需一次性地将语料库组织成 Corpus Atlas（语料图谱），每个查询在此共享结构上自适应导航，而非重建类似结构。该方法核心思想是将大规模智能体搜索形式化为在可复用语料库结构上的有限预算导航问题。

Result: 在 BrowseComp-Plus 基准上，AtlasNav 达到 92.05% 的严格准确率，相对先前动态工作区最先进方法减少 30.21% 的在线推理成本。在匹配预算下，它更早实现完整必需证据，更快逼近同一模型的证据供给经验参考。该表示原则在 PhantomWiki 的不同语料库组织和受控 10K-1M 缩放下依然有效，并竞争性地迁移到异构企业知识场景。

Conclusion: 智能体搜索不仅依赖于可访问的证据，还取决于语料库的表示方式——合适的表示能使有限的交互成为有效的导航。持久化的语料库结构预组织比在线重构更为高效，这一原则在多个基准和不同规模的语料库上均被验证有效。

Abstract: Large language model agents are moving beyond conventional retrieval-augmented generation toward direct interaction with external corpora. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) keeps the full corpus accessible, yet reachable evidence can remain unusable under finite interaction budgets. Required evidence may fail to surface, a surfaced supporting document may remain unopened, or an opened document may fail to expose its decisive fragment. We call this progressive silent loss Evidence Blindness and quantify it through stage-wise evidence realization. Within the DCI paradigm, raw interaction adds little reusable corpus organization, while dynamic-workspace methods reconstruct a query-conditioned interaction space from each query and trajectory. In both cases, useful structure is recovered largely online. We instead formulate large-scale agentic search as finite-budget navigation over reusable corpus structure. We introduce AtlasNav, a persistent multi-view corpus-navigation framework that retains direct corpus interaction but organizes the corpus once into a Corpus Atlas, allowing each query to navigate adaptively rather than reconstruct shared structure. On BrowseComp-Plus, AtlasNav achieves 92.05% strict accuracy while reducing recorded online inference cost by 30.21% relative to the prior dynamic-workspace state of the art. Under matched budgets, it realizes the complete required evidence earlier and approaches the same model's evidence-supplied empirical reference more rapidly. The same representation principle remains effective under PhantomWiki's distinct corpus organization and controlled 10K-1M scaling, and transfers competitively to heterogeneous enterprise knowledge. These results show that agentic search depends not only on accessible evidence, but also on how the corpus is represented so that limited interaction becomes effective navigation.

</details>


### [271] [StepGuard: Learning Step-Level Guardrails with Scalable Supervision and Safety-Utility Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24777)
*Zhijie Zheng,Yu Li,Chen Qian,Yuqian Fu,Yanwei Fu,Lu Sheng,Jing Shao,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StepGuard，一个步骤级的智能体护栏模型，能够在工具执行前审计代理轨迹并检查动作安全性，同时引入StepGen自动数据引擎生成安全/不安全轨迹对以及Balance-GRPO算法平衡过防御与欠防御问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有护栏模型大多评估完整轨迹而非执行前的步骤级动作监控，导致预执行阶段的安全性检查未被充分探索，存在文件修改、信息泄露和未授权操作等安全风险。

Method: 1) StepGuard：步骤级护栏模型，既能审计已完成轨迹，也能在工具执行前检查动作；2) StepGen：自动数据引擎，在相同上下文下生成仅风险步骤动作不同的安全/不安全轨迹对；3) Balance-GRPO：动态平衡安全与不安全动作学习的强化学习算法，基于观测准确率调整。

Result: StepGuard在开放权重护栏模型中取得最高平均准确率，性能与GPT-5.4相当；在AgentDojo和AgentDyn上，相比无护栏设置平均攻击成功率降低77.3%，而平均效用仅下降2.8个百分点。

Conclusion: StepGuard在有效防御攻击（显著降低攻击成功率）的同时保持了极小的效用损失，证明了步骤级护栏在保障LLM智能体安全方面的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents can interact with external environments through tool invocation, but this capability also introduces security risks such as file modification, information leakage, and unauthorized actions. Existing guardrails often evaluate completed trajectories, leaving pre-execution monitoring of step-level actions underexplored. We propose StepGuard, a step-level guard model that can audit completed agent trajectories and check tool actions before they are executed. To train StepGuard, we introduce StepGen, an automatic data engine that generates safe and unsafe trajectories with the same context but different actions at the risky step. To further reduce over-defense and under-defense, we propose Balance-GRPO, which dynamically balances learning between safe and unsafe actions based on their observed accuracy. Experiments show that StepGuard achieves the highest average accuracy among open-weight guard models, with performance comparable to GPT-5.4. When used to guard agents on AgentDojo and AgentDyn, StepGuard reduces mean attack success rate by 77.3% relative to the no-guard setting, while mean utility drops by only 2.8 percentage points.

</details>


### [272] [Right Diagnoses, Decorative Reasoning:A Perturbation Audit of Medical Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24790)
*Mengzhu Xu,Jifan Gao,Xia Jiang,Yaoxin Wu,Xi Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过30种临床动机扰动算子（严重度反转、否定翻转、人口变更、证据剔除以）对14个LLM在4个医学QA基准上进行系统性审计，发现医学思维链（CoT）存在严重"解耦"现象：Chain-Decoupling Rate高达72.9%，即链无法反映编辑且答案不随之翻转；破坏链不改变准确率、移除CoT提示也不降低性能。两位认证临床医生复核确认98.5%的扰动问题仍保持金标准答案有效。该现象在医学/推理微调及规模变化下均成立，闭源模型中答案侧信号也一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT忠实性检测工具面向通用领域，未考虑临床代价；而医学LLM评估将思维链视为黑盒。临床医生依赖CoT作为医学推理的证据，但其是否真正反映模型决策从未被严格检验，亟需一个考虑临床语境的审计框架。

Method: 构建医学扰动审计框架：(1) 30种临床动机算子同时编辑链和问题；(2) 配对链-更新×答案-翻转联合分析分类各模型的失败模式；(3) 引入Chain-Decoupling Rate (CDR)指标；(4) 在14个LLM×4个医学QA基准上开展三项独立检验，并由两位委员会认证临床医生复核扰动问题的金标准有效性。

Result: CDR在临床意义破坏性编辑上达72.9%；链损坏后准确率不变；移除CoT提示准确率不下降。医生复核98.5%扰动保留金标准。现象跨医学/推理微调及模型规模一致，闭源模型答案侧信号同样印证解耦。

Conclusion: 医学CoT在很大程度上是"仅文档化"而非忠实推理证据，本文的框架与CDR可作为衡量医学CoT忠实性的可复用标尺，警示临床用户不应将可见思维链视为模型真实推理的可信依据。

Abstract: Clinicians read chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales as evidence of medical reasoning, but whether the visible chain plays that role is rarely tested. General-domain CoT-faithfulness probes ignore clinical cost, and medical LLM evaluations treat the chain as a black box. We close this gap with a medical perturbation audit: a 30-operator battery edits both the chain and the question with clinically motivated operators (severity reversal, negation flip, demographic swap, evidence ablation), paired with a chain-update times answer-flip joint analysis that classifies each model by its failure mode. Applied to 14 LLMs on four medical QA benchmarks, three independent tests converge: the Chain-Decoupling Rate (CDR; chain does not register the edit and the answer does not flip) is 72.9% panel-wide on clinically meaningful destructive edits, chain corruption leaves accuracy unchanged, and removing CoT prompting does not reduce accuracy. Two board-certified clinicians re-annotate N=197 perturbed questions; 98.5% leave the gold defensible. The pattern holds across medical and reasoning fine-tuning and scale; on the closed-source tier, where the chain text is unavailable, the answer-side signals are consistent with the same decoupling. Our framework and CDR provide a reusable yardstick for auditing whether medical CoT is faithful or merely documentation.

</details>


### [273] [CAFE: Self-Improving Search Agents Need Co-Evolving Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24794)
*Boyang Liu,Senjie Jin,Peixin Wang,Zhangyue Yin,Yibo Wang,Yuhao Zhou,Xinbing Liang,Shizheng Zhu,Yuhui Wang,Jingqi Tong,Zhiheng Xi,Jiazheng Zhang,Clive Bai,Clarenceai,Blaze Chen,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAFE（耦合智能体-反馈演化）框架通过共享参数模型在搜索智能体和评论家角色间交替切换，结合在线强化学习和离线偏好优化，使反馈与策略共同演化，在七个智能体搜索基准上平均超越现有RL方法，并在所有六个域外基准上保持优势，同时减少答案级幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结果监督的搜索智能体只能获得终端奖励，无法定位中间错误或在错误累积前纠正轨迹。理想的做法是将纠正性反馈视为轨迹内干预，但这一耦合问题具有双重挑战：智能体需要决定何时请求和使用反馈，而评论家需要从结果混淆的轨迹中推断有用纠正——尤其当失败模式随智能体进步而改变时。

Method: CAFE框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 从基础智能体自身失败轨迹初始化反馈条件恢复能力；(2) 在线RL阶段，通过提示级调用-跳过成功差距形成比较性反馈估计来塑造请求回报，并使用反馈感知的优势塑造在反馈前后对token优势重新加权；(3) 离线阶段，通过从匹配的成功/失败轨迹中推导的偏好优化来学习反馈。共享参数模型在搜索智能体和评论家角色间交替训练。

Result: 在七个智能体搜索基准上平均优于评估的基于RL的搜索智能体；在所有六个域外基准上保持性能增益；减少了答案级幻觉。消融实验显示只改进智能体或只改进评论家最终都会出现平台期，而交替更新两者能持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 自改进的搜索智能体需要与其引导的策略共同演化的反馈。这一发现表明，反馈和目标策略的协同演化是打破性能瓶颈的关键，单纯优化一方都无法实现持续进步。

Abstract: Outcome-supervised search agents learn when and how to retrieve evidence, but terminal rewards neither localize intermediate errors nor redirect an ongoing trajectory before those errors compound. Treating corrective feedback as a learned in-trajectory intervention couples the two roles: the agent must decide when to request and use feedback, while the critic must infer useful corrections from outcome-confounded rollouts whose failure patterns shift as the agent improves. We introduce CAFE (Coupled Agent--Feedback Evolution), a framework in which a shared-parameter model alternates between search-agent and critic roles. CAFE initializes feedback-conditioned recovery from trajectories built around the base agent's own failures, then couples online and offline optimization. During online RL, a comparative feedback estimate uses a prompt-level call--skip success gap to shape request returns, while feedback-aware advantage shaping reweights token advantages before and after feedback. Offline, rollout-derived preference optimization learns feedback from matched successful and unsuccessful trajectories. On seven agentic search benchmarks, CAFE outperforms the evaluated RL-based search agents on average, retains its gains across all six out-of-domain benchmarks, and reduces answer-level hallucinations. One-sided ablations show that improving only the agent or only the critic eventually plateaus, whereas alternating the two updates continues to improve performance. These findings suggest that a self-improving search agent needs feedback that co-evolves with the policy it guides.

</details>


### [274] [Strictly Causal Streaming Video Anomaly Detection with a Theoretically-Grounded State-Space Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24810)
*Yogesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种严格因果的流式视频异常检测器，基于对角线性状态空间递归和输入/状态依赖的衰减门，以O(1)时间和内存每帧更新，无需片段缓冲或前瞻。作者推导了递归衰减谱与检测延迟/可捕获最短异常之间的闭式关系，并在UCSD Ped2和CUHK Avenue上验证。在Apple M3 Pro硬件上实测每帧0.74-0.77ms（1300+ FPS），但帧级AUC（67.9%/70.2%）落后于非因果SSM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Mamba风格SSM的视频异常检测方法仍依赖内部片段/窗口缓冲，缺乏时间记忆与检测延迟关系的理论解释，且只在GPU吞吐量上评估效率而非目标边缘硬件。

Method: 采用严格因果流式架构，核心为带输入和状态依赖衰减门的对角线性状态空间递归，在冻结视觉骨干上通过因果下一嵌入预测自监督训练。推导衰减谱与检测延迟/最短检测异常之间的闭式关系，并在真实边缘硬件（Apple M3 Pro）上实测端到端延迟与吞吐。

Result: 在UCSD Ped2和CUHK Avenue上，预测的稳定延迟界（57-59帧）远高于实测检测延迟（1.6和18.4帧），表明事件边界门而非基础衰减决定响应速度。M3 Pro上每帧0.74-0.77ms（1300+ FPS）。帧级AUC为67.9%（Ped2）和70.2%（Avenue），落后于非因果SSM基线。消融显示衰减门作用依赖数据集规模，在小Ped2训练集上有害、在大Avenue上有益。

Conclusion: 该流式方法在边缘硬件效率和理论延迟分析上有优势，但精度低于非因果SSM基线。未来工作需缩小精度差距，并在更大基准上扩展评估。

Abstract: Recent work has applied Mamba style state space models (SSMs) to video anomaly detection, yet existing approaches still rely on buffering clips or windows internally, lack a theoretical account of how temporal memory relates to detection latency, and benchmark efficiency only through GPU throughput rather than the edge hardware these methods are intended to target. We introduce a strictly causal streaming anomaly detector whose fixed size state is updated in O(1) time and memory per incoming frame, with no lookahead and no clip buffering. Its temporal core is a diagonal linear state space recurrence with an input and state dependent decay gate, trained self supervised through causal next embedding prediction on a frozen visual backbone. We derive a closed form relationship between the recurrence decay spectrum and both detection delay and the shortest anomaly it can reliably capture, then validate empirically on UCSD Ped2 and CUHK Avenue. The settling delay bound predicted from the learned base decay (57 to 59 frames) sits far above the measured detection delay (1.6 and 18.4 frames), showing that the event boundary gate, not the base decay, governs responsiveness. We further report end to end latency and throughput measured directly on Apple M3 Pro hardware, 0.74 ms and 0.77 ms per frame (over 1300 FPS), rather than simulated GPU numbers. With an untuned initial configuration the method reaches 67.9 percent and 70.2 percent frame level AUC on Ped2 and Avenue, trailing prior non causal SSM baselines in accuracy. Ablations over decay rate, state size, and gating reveal that the gate contribution is dataset size dependent, hurting accuracy on the smaller Ped2 training set but helping on the larger Avenue one. Closing this accuracy gap and extending evaluation to a third, larger benchmark are immediate next steps.

</details>


### [275] [Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge for LLM-Based Knowledge Graph QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24824)
*Emanuel Kitzelmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CES-PK（Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge）框架，用于在大语言模型进行知识图谱问答时，通过轻量级符号约束验证候选答案，在不依赖完整语义解析为可执行查询的情况下，利用三值约束语义（满足/违反/未知）在开放世界假设下过滤无效答案并为有效答案提供符号证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于KGQA时存在两类现有方法的不足：一是完全依赖语义解析生成SPARQL等可执行查询，因真实KG模式复杂或不完整而脆弱；二是LLM直接在KG上推理生成答案，虽然更鲁棒但缺乏形式化保证。需要一个互补的中间方案。

Method: 提出CES-PK问题形式化：LLM生成候选答案后，从问题中推导出轻量级符号约束（类型约束、关系约束、排除约束），采用三值逻辑（满足/违反/未知）评估约束，因KG不完整而允许未知状态以避免在开放世界假设下错误拒绝有效答案。

Result: 在Hetionet生物医学知识图谱上实例化该框架，实验表明通过过滤无效候选可提升精确率，同时通过保留未被明确违反约束的候选来保持召回率；约束满足的候选获得正向符号证据，可用于对剩余候选进行排序。

Conclusion: 符号约束验证是LLM语义解析与自由推理之间的有效补充路径，三值语义处理不完备KG是可行且有效的，为KGQA提供了一种兼具鲁棒性与一定形式保证的方案。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for knowledge graph question answering (KGQA), but can fail to correctly ground answers in the underlying graph. Current approaches to LLM-based KGQA either rely on full semantic parsing into executable queries such as SPARQL, which is brittle in practice due to complex schemas or incompleteness of real-world KGs, or on LLM-reasoning and answer generation over KGs, which can be more robust but lacks formal guarantees. In this work, we study a complementary setting in which \emph{candidate} answers are generated by an LLM-based system and subsequently verified using lightweight symbolic constraints derived from the question. We introduce \emph{Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge (CES-PK)}, a problem formulation that focuses on eliminating invalid answers and providing symbolic support for valid ones without requiring construction of executable logical forms. To account for incomplete KGs, we employ a three-valued constraint semantics (\emph{satisfied, violated, unknown}) that avoids incorrect rejections under open-world assumptions. To demonstrate the effects of our method, we instantiate this framework over the Hetionet biomedical knowledge graph and evaluate the impact of type, relation, and exclusion constraints. Experiments show that precision improves by filtering invalid candidates, while recall is preserved due to retaining candidates whose constraints are not explicitly violated. Satisfied constraints provide additional positive symbolic evidence to rank remaining candidates.

</details>


### [276] [A Dual-Dimensional LLM Framework for Automated Item Incidental Content Similarity Analysis in Large-Scale Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24825)
*Jing Huang,Jihong Zhang,Hua-Hua Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的双维度自动化试题相似性分析框架（AISA），通过"结构化分解"与"语义关联度"两个维度捕捉试题间非构念相关的冗余性。心理测量学验证表明LLM指标比传统文本相似度指标更贴合构念无关局部依赖指标，且在CAT模拟中，基于LLM相似度约束的选题策略能提升估计稳定性、降低偏差，效率损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模测评的扩展与自动试题生成技术的普及，导致试题中出现大量非刻意的内容冗余（如措辞或情境框架的重复）。传统相似度指标（如BLEU、余弦相似度）无法同时捕捉驱动冗余感知的微妙结构层与语义层，亟需更精细的相似性分析方法。

Method: 提出AISA框架，利用LLM从两个维度操作化试题相似性：（1）结构化分解（Structured Decomposition）——解析试题的结构组成；（2）语义关联度（Semantic Relatedness）——评估语义层面的关联。随后进行心理测量学验证，并在CAT中通过加入LLM相似度约束的选题策略进行模拟评估。

Result: 心理测量验证显示LLM派生指标比传统文本指标更接近构念无关局部依赖的指标，并产生更一致的试题参数分组。CAT模拟表明，加入LLM相似度约束的选题算法比基于传统指标的约束能更好地提升估计稳定性、减少偏差，且效率折损很小。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的AISA有望支持大规模题库管理、内容感知的试卷组卷，以及体验敏感的适应性测试，在多种测评情境中具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of large-scale assessments and the growing adoption of automatic item generation have intensified concerns about incidental content redundancy, where construct-irrelevant elements such as wording or contextual framing become unintentionally repetitive across items. Traditional similarity metrics like BLEU or cosine similarity, often fail to capture the nuanced structural and semantic layers that drive perceived redundancy simultaneously. This study proposes a dual-dimensional framework for Automated Item Similarity Analysis (AISA) powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), operationalizing similarity through Structured Decomposition and Semantic Relatedness. Psychometric validation indicates that LLM-derived metrics align more closely with indicators of construct-irrelevant local dependence and yield more coherent item parameter groupings than traditional text-based measures. The framework is further evaluated through its application in Computerized Adaptive Testing (CAT). Simulations reveal that incorporating LLM-based similarity constraints into item selection improves estimation stability and reduces bias with minimal efficiency trade-offs, outperforming constraints based on conventional metrics. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-powered AISA to support scalable bank curation, content-aware test assembly, and experience-sensitive adaptive testing across diverse assessment contexts.

</details>


### [277] [FedV-KGQA: Multi-Hop Question Answering over Vertically Partitioned Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24846)
*Md Saikat Islam Khan Bappy,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedV-KGQA框架，用于在垂直分区（各组织共享实体但拥有不相交的关系集）的知识图谱上进行多跳问答推理。该方法通过本地图增强和知识图谱嵌入确保原始三元组和关系参数不离开各数据孤岛，并引入主题实体锚定机制避免运行时跨孤岛通信。实验评估了12种模型配置，在三个基准上表现接近集中式性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中知识图谱问答数据常因治理和数据主权约束而分散在不同组织之间，但现有集中式系统无法在垂直分区（各组织共享实体但持有不同关系）的情况下回答需要跨孤岛事实的多跳问题。

Method: 1) 本地图增强（local graph enrichment）：在各孤岛内推展本地图结构；2) 知识图谱嵌入：确保原始三元组和关系参数不离开各孤岛，建立结构数据边界；3) 主题实体锚定机制（topic entity anchoring）：将问题锚定到正确的图邻域，无需运行时跨孤岛通信。

Result: 在三个基准上评估12种模型配置，FedV-KGQA表现优异，接近集中式性能，可泛化到3跳推理，并对嵌入扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedV-KGQA证明了在垂直分区知识图谱上进行无需跨孤岛通信的多跳问答推理是可行的，能够在保护数据主权的同时保持接近集中式的性能。

Abstract: Real-world data for knowledge graph question answering is often distributed across different organizations due to governance and data sovereignty constraints. While centralized systems exist, they cannot answer multi-hop questions when the required facts are split across vertically partitioned silos. In this paper, we propose FedV-KGQA, a framework for multi-hop reasoning over knowledge graphs in which organizations share entities but own disjoint sets of relations. Our approach combines local graph enrichment and knowledge graph embeddings to ensure raw triples and relation parameters never leave each silo, establishing a structural data boundary without requiring centralized graph access. We further introduce a topic entity anchoring mechanism that grounds questions in the correct graph neighborhood without any runtime inter-silo communication. We evaluate 12 model configurations across three benchmarks and show that FedV-KGQA performs strongly, remains close to centralized performance, generalizes to 3-hop reasoning, and is robust to embedding perturbations.

</details>


### [278] [SPO++: Stream-Aligned Policy Optimization for Asynchronous Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24870)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Qianshan Wei,Ziqi Zhou,Zihe Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPO++，一种单流策略优化方法，通过修正轨迹级白化与token级actor损失之间的不匹配，并按照生成策略事件而非接收顺序组织提示证据，提升了在线学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组相对强化学习需要等待同一提示的兄弟rollout，这对于长且可变的工具使用轨迹成本高昂。SPO通过持久提示级价值估计消除了这一依赖，但其"每条轨迹白化一个优势"的配方无法正确居中actor消费的token加权数量，导致优化偏差。

Method: SPO++包含两个关键改进：(1) 在动作token测度下对终端结果优势进行标准化，而非简单的轨迹中心化，以修复token加权数量与轨迹级白化之间的不匹配；(2) 按生成提示证据的策略事件（而非学习者接收顺序）组织提示，以更好地利用prompt证据。

Result: 在ALFWorld两个模型规模和Math-TIR上，SPO++相比SPO在匹配运行条件下提升了在线学习效率。配对消融实验表明，动作token测度归一化是最强的测试组件。

Conclusion: SPO++通过修正优势归一化的测度不匹配问题，并重新组织提示证据，实现了比SPO更高效的在线单流策略优化，验证了token测度标准化在强化学习中的关键作用。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning waits for sibling rollouts of the same prompt, which is costly for long and variable tool-use trajectories. Single-stream Policy Optimization (SPO) removes this dependency with a persistent prompt-level value estimate, but its recipe whitens one advantage per trajectory before optimizing a token-mean actor loss. We show that trajectory centering generally does not center the token-weighted quantity consumed by the actor, and fix the mismatch by standardizing terminal-outcome advantages under the action-token measure. We additionally organize prompt evidence by the policy event that generated it rather than learner receipt order. Across matched runs on ALFWorld at two model scales and on Math-TIR, SPO++ improves online learning efficiency over SPO. A paired ablation identifies action-token-measure normalization as the strongest tested component.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [279] [Task-disentangled Low-Rank Adaptation for Versatile Audio-visual Multi-modal Learning Tasks within a Unified Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24209)
*Hanyu Xuan,Mengqi Zhang,Junjun Mao,Fei Wang,Kun Li,Guanghui Yue,Zhiliang Wu,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的音频-视觉多模态学习框架，通过任务解耦的LoRA机制（包含任务通用低秩矩阵、任务特定调制矩阵和跨任务协作专家）实现多任务协同训练，既超越了现有统一模型，也在某些任务上优于任务特定模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AVMML研究大多孤立地处理每个任务，与人类统一认知能力不符。然而简单地联合训练多个AVMML任务会因任务间复杂关系产生相互干扰，因此需要设计一个能同时处理多种AVMML任务的统一框架。

Method: 利用大语言模型的强大表征与泛化能力，设计任务解耦的LoRA机制，包含三个组件：任务通用低秩矩阵（捕获通用音视频知识）、任务特定调制矩阵（解耦任务特定模式）、跨任务协作专家（利用任务间内在关联），从模型和任务两个层面实现显式协作。

Result: 该框架在多个AVMML任务上超越了现有统一音频-视觉模型，并且在某些AVMML任务上优于大多数任务特定模型。

Conclusion: 通过任务解耦LoRA实现多任务间的动态知识整合与有效协作，验证了统一多任务AVMML框架的可行性和优势。

Abstract: Inspired by human multi-modal perception, Audio-Visual Multi-Modal Learning (AVMML) integrates auditory and visual information to leverage complementary cross-modal cues, enabling more robust and comprehensive scene perception. Existing studies predominantly tackle each AVMML task in isolation, which stands in stark contrast to humans' unified cognitive capacity for handling versatile perception. However, naive joint training across multiple AVMML tasks often suffers from mutual interference, arising from the intricate inter-task relationships. To address this, we propose a unified framework that simultaneously accommodates versatile AVMML tasks. Specifically, benefiting from powerful representation and generalization capabilities of large language models, we design a task-disentangled Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) mechanism that enables dynamic integration of both task-specific and task-shared knowledge, thereby facilitating effective multi-task collaboration. The proposed task-disentangled LoRA comprises three components: a task-general low-rank matrix, task-specific modulation matrices, and cross-task collaboration experts, which respectively capture universal audio-visual knowledge, decouple task-specific pattern, and exploit inherent inter-task correlations. By unifying explicit collaboration from both model and task perspectives, our approach not only surpasses existing unified audio-visual models across multiple AVMML tasks, but also outperforms most task-specific models on certain AVMML tasks.

</details>


### [280] [EVEREST:Endogenous Vision-Language Reinforcement Reasoning Exploration for Urban Socio-Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24640)
*Qixiu Li,Zhongzhi He,Xiang Zhu,Xiaoyong Li,Jiarun Lin,Weifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出EVEREST模型，采用以自我为中心的探索策略，让模型主动探查边界线索并进行自我纠正，同时将自然语言提示形式化为伪代码以规范执行逻辑，并通过强化学习实现该过程，在城市社会语义分割任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市社会语义分割方法虽达到较高精度，但在目标边界刻画上不准确，根本原因在于模型仅依赖被动聚合的全局跨模态线索，缺乏对环境的主动探索。

Method: 1) 提出EVEREST模型，采用以自我为中心的探索策略（egocentric exploration strategy），使模型主动调查边界线索并进行自我纠正；2) 将离散自然语言提示形式化为伪代码（pseudocode）以规范化执行逻辑；3) 利用强化学习来实现这一不可约过程，激发模型的结构化推理能力。

Result: 在真实世界城市社会语义数据集上，EVEREST在所有指标上均取得最优性能，验证了模型的优越性。

Conclusion: 通过主动探索与自我纠正策略替代被动跨模态线索聚合，能有效改善城市社会语义分割中的边界刻画精度，为数字与卫星影像融合的语义分割任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: Urban socio-semantic segmentation leverages digital and satellite imagery to provide critical spatial semantic information for downstream applications such as urban resource allocation. Although existing methods achieve high segmentation accuracy, they still suffer from inaccurate delineation of target boundaries. The underlying issue is that current models primarily rely on passively aggregated global cross-modal cues, lacking active exploration of the environment. To address this limitation, we propose the EVEREST model, which adopts an egocentric exploration strategy that enables the model to actively investigate boundary cues and perform self-correction. In addition, we formulate discrete natural-language prompts as pseudocode to regularize the execution logic. Reinforcement learning is further employed to implement this irreducible process and elicit the model's structured reasoning capability. Our EVEREST achieves optimal performance on all metrics in the real world urban socio-semantic dataset, demonstrating the superiority of our model. Codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/EVEREST-9D21/.

</details>


### [281] [Learning to Prefer Reliably: Error-Augmented Emotion Preference Optimization with Calibrated Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24730)
*Zilong Huang,Junyi Peng,Junjie Li,Kai Li,Wenze Ren,Kong Aik Lee,Man-Wai Mak,Tatsuya Kawahara*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出EAPO框架，通过构建错误增强数据集（从每个正向描述生成多个受控的情感感知负向描述）和采用多MLLM评委的边际校准软融合策略，分别在数据层面和模型层面提升多模态大语言模型的情感偏好判断可靠性。实验证明EAPO在MER2026-EmoPrefer挑战赛数据集上显著改善了情感偏好预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感偏好学习存在两个关键局限：(1) 数据层面，传统成对监督稀疏，通常每个正向描述仅有一个负向描述，模型难以充分接触语义流畅但情感不一致的描述；(2) 模型层面，依赖单一MLLM评委，其判断可能受模型特定偏见影响，尤其在处理细粒度或模糊的多模态情感线索时。

Method: EAPO框架包含两个核心组件：首先构建错误增强数据集，从每个首选描述生成多个受控且情感感知的负向描述，丰富监督信号；然后采用多独立MLLM评委的集成策略，通过边际校准软融合将不同评委的异构偏好边际映射到统一尺度后再进行聚合，从而提升判断鲁棒性。

Result: 在MER2026-EmoPrefer挑战赛数据集及所构建的错误增强数据集上的实验表明，EAPO显著提升了情感偏好预测性能，并增强了MLLM评委在评估与视频多模态情感证据相冲突的流畅描述时的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EAPO通过数据增强和多评委融合双重策略有效解决了情感偏好学习中监督稀疏和单模型偏见的问题，为MLLM-based情感偏好判断提供了一套更可靠的框架，代码已开源。

Abstract: Emotion preference learning uses pairwise comparisons between candidate descriptions to align multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with human judgments of open-ended emotion descriptions and to train reward models that capture human emotional preferences. However, conventional pairwise supervision is often sparse, typically providing only a single negative description for each positive description, and therefore offers limited coverage of the diverse ways in which an emotion description can be incorrect. In particular, models may be insufficiently exposed to semantically fluent but emotionally inconsistent descriptions. Beyond this data-level limitation, relying on a single MLLM judge introduces a distinct model-level concern: its judgments can be affected by model-specific biases when interpreting fine-grained or ambiguous multimodal emotional cues. To address these limitations, we propose Error-Augmented Preference Optimization (EAPO), a framework for improving the reliability of MLLM-based emotion preference judgment at both the data and model levels. First, we construct an error-augmented dataset by generating multiple controlled and emotion-aware negative descriptions from each preferred description. We then adapt multiple independent MLLM judges to this richer supervision and aggregate their preference margins using margin-calibrated soft fusion, which maps heterogeneous margins to a common scale before aggregation. Experiments on the MER2026-EmoPrefer Challenge dataset and our error-augmented dataset demonstrate that EAPO improves emotion preference prediction and enhances the robustness of MLLM judges when evaluating fluent descriptions that conflict with the video's multimodal emotional evidence. Our code is available at https://github.com/slash1028/EAPO-EmoPrefer.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [282] [Equivariant Cellular Sheaves for Molecular Electronic Structure: Bridging Sheaf Cohomology and E(3)-Equivariant Hamiltonian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23571)
*Krishna Harish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全新的结构观察：在局域原子轨道基下，分子的单粒子哈密顿量经常数平移后，恰好是分子所构造的规则胞腔复形上的细胞搁层（cellular sheaf）的拉普拉斯算子。通过将限制映射设为O(3)-等变的双中心核，可恢复Slater-Koster形式为特例，从而得到E(3)与置换等变的算子。这一形式化带来了拓扑不变量（零阶上同调对应非键轨道）、H^1携带环与离域信息，并严格推广了现有的等变消息传递网络与CW网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的等变消息传递网络和CW网络在分子性质预测上已取得进展，但缺乏对分子电子结构深层拓扑结构的建模。作者的核心动机是发现分子哈密顿量与细胞搁层拉普拉斯算子之间的深层结构对应关系，从而利用代数拓扑与搁层理论为等变分子建模提供新的不变量和更丰富的几何信息表达。

Method: 1) 将分子单粒子哈密顿量经正半定平移后映射为细胞搁层的拉普拉斯算子；2) 用O(3)-可转向的双中心核来构造限制映射（restriction maps），以Slater-Koster形式为特例，保证E(3)与置换等变性；3) 利用搁层的零阶上同调H^0（等于零模轨道）和Hodge 1-拉普拉斯算子（通过H^1携带环和离域信息）提供拓扑不变特性；4) 构建Equivariant Cellular Sheaf Networks（ECSN）模型。

Result: 实验验证表明：哈密顿量到搁层的嵌入达到机器精度；上同调维度在11个共轭分子上重建了非键轨道计数；搁层拉普拉斯算子的O(3)等变性达到机器精度；等变模型在方向性电子目标上取得更低误差和更好的旋转泛化性能。同时证明了模型的等变性、表达力和上同调对应关系。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是提出了分子哈密顿量-细胞搁层的数学形式化及其拓扑不变量，而非等变哈密顿量预测本身。该工作为分子深度学习开辟了基于代数拓扑的新视角，将经典量子化学结果（如非键轨道计数）与拓扑不变量建立联系，并为未来的等变分子建模提供了更丰富的理论工具。

Abstract: Equivariant message-passing networks are the standard model for molecular property and interatomic-potential prediction, and recent work predicts the electronic Hamiltonian itself in an E(3)-equivariant way. Separately, topological deep learning has extended graph networks to cellular sheaves. Our central observation is structural: in a localized atomic-orbital basis, the molecular single-particle Hamiltonian, after a constant shift that makes it positive semidefinite, is the Laplacian of a cellular sheaf on a regular cell complex built from the molecule. Making the restriction maps O(3)-steerable two-center kernels from bond geometry recovers the Slater-Koster form as a special case and yields an E(3)- and permutation-equivariant operator. Three consequences follow. First, the zeroth sheaf cohomology H^0 = ker L is a topological invariant equal to the non-bonding (zero-mode) orbitals, recovering the classical alternant non-bonding-orbital count as a lower bound. Second, the Hodge 1-Laplacian lets higher cells (rings) carry cycle and delocalization information through H^1. Third, the model strictly generalizes E(3)-equivariant message-passing networks and CW networks, and inherits the anti-oversmoothing of non-trivial sheaf diffusion. We prove equivariance, expressivity, and cohomological-correspondence results for the Equivariant Cellular Sheaf Networks, and validate them numerically: the Hamiltonian-to-sheaf embedding is exact to machine precision, the cohomology dimension reproduces non-bonding-orbital counts across eleven conjugated molecules, the sheaf Laplacian is O(3)-equivariant to machine precision, and the equivariant model attains lower error and rotation generalization on a directional electronic target. Our contribution is this sheaf-theoretic formalization and its invariants, not equivariant Hamiltonian prediction itself.

</details>


### [283] [Data Predictability Shapes Weibull Weight-Scale Growth in Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23573)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现训练后的Transformer权重幅值可用两参数Weibull分布（形状k≈1.2稳定）概括，尺度λ携带主要训练变化。通过训练前可计算的二元条件熵D=H(next|prev)，发现λ²-λ₀²=C₀(η)+C₁(η)(H_r-D)^0.59的学习率条件定律。移除每η两个系数后，跨23个运行（学习率跨越一个数量级）的数据以单位斜率坍缩到(H_r-D)^0.59（R²=0.941）。该定律是前向预测器，端到端自验证恢复held-out权重增长误差仅5.7%，且跨模型和逐层分辨率均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语料属性如何决定Transformer训练中权重幅度的增长，建立训练前可预测训练后权重行为的理论框架，并探索更广泛的Φ(D,R,A,H)数据到权重映射关系。

Method: 使用两参数Weibull分布拟合权重幅度（形状k≈1.2固定，只追踪尺度λ）；以训练前计算的二元条件熵D=H(next|prev)为预测变量；通过受控语料破坏家族建立学习率条件化定律λ²-λ₀²=C₀(η)+C₁(η)(H_r-D)^0.59，其中指数0.59从独立测量的数据侧饱和关系中继承而非直接拟合。

Result: 23个运行（学习率跨一个数量级）在移除每η两个系数后以单位斜率坍缩到(H_r-D)^0.59（R²=0.941），优于直接每η拟合（R²≈0.82）；端到端自验证的前向预测相对误差仅5.7%；定律在模型和逐层分辨率以及两种架构下成立，仅系数变化；跨语料预测对代码过度预测，揭示了冗余度作为第二轴。

Conclusion: 二元条件熵D是一个强大的训练前统计量，能以前向方式预测Transformer权重增长。所发现的定律具有清晰的边界：它在本语料族内有效，但跨语料预测失败暗示需要纳入冗余度R作为第二轴，指向更一般的Φ(D,R,A,H)数据到权重框架。

Abstract: A trained transformer's weight magnitudes can be summarized by a two-parameter Weibull distribution whose shape $k \approx 1.2$ is stable across layers and models, so the scale $λ$ carries most training-induced movement. What corpus property sets how much $λ$ grows? Using the bigram conditional entropy $D = H(\text{next} \mid \text{prev})$, a training-free statistic computed before training, we find across controlled corruption families a learning-rate-conditioned law, $λ^2 - λ_0^2 = C_0(η) + C_1(η)(H_r - D)^{0.59}$, where $H_r$ is a matched-budget shuffle baseline. The convex exponent is inherited from an independently measured data-side saturation relation rather than fitted directly to the growth curve. After removing the two per-$η$ coefficients, 23 runs spanning an order of magnitude in learning rate collapse onto $(H_r - D)^{0.59}$ with unit slope ($R^2 = 0.941$; direct per-$η$ fits are weaker, $R^2 \approx 0.82$). Because $D$ is computed before training, the law is a forward predictor: an end-to-end self-validation recovers held-out within-family weight growth with 5.7% relative error. The readout holds at model and per-layer resolutions and across two tested architectures, with the functional form preserved and only the coefficients changing. It also marks its boundary: cross-corpus prediction over-predicts code, implicating redundancy as a second axis of a broader $Φ(D,R,A,H)$ data-to-weight framework.

</details>


### [284] [From Causal Plausibility to Causal Reliability: Evaluating LLMs as Calibrated Direct Causal-Edge Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23660)
*Amit Kumar,Elnur Adl Zarabi,Suranjana Trivedy,Zhiqian Chen,Lei Zhang,Kaiqun Fu,Taoran Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了12个指令微调的开源大语言模型在结构因果发现中的表现，发现LLM的因果判断存在强烈的"高召回偏向"（过度预测密集图、大量假阳性边）、难以区分直接因果与间接/方向性关系，且传统置信度估计不可靠，而"共识"（cross-prompt/cross-model agreement）是更有前景的置信度信号。作者建议将LLM视为外部验证过的软因果先验来源，而非因果结构的直接证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于为结构因果发现提供先验因果知识，但其直接边判断和置信度是否可信尚不明确，需要系统性的实证评估。

Method: 评估12个指令微调开源模型，覆盖6个基准因果图、5种提示策略和4种置信度来源（口头化、基于logit、跨提示一致性、跨模型一致性），采用纯语言成对比较协议，并进行基准熟悉度审计。

Result: 三大关键发现：(i) LLM因果判断强烈偏向高召回，预测图密集且假阳性多，提示主要改变精确率-召回率权衡而非解决过度预测，模型规模收益在最大图上递减且不能消除校准偏差；(ii) LLM能捕捉因果关联性但难以识别直接性或方向性，40.0%间接边和36.0%反向非边被误分类为直接边，且80.8%/84.6%的假阳性获得>=80%的口头置信度，存在严重过度自信；(iii) 传统置信度估计不可靠（logit置信度常塌缩至接近1.0），而跨提示/跨模型一致性有更好的均值校准和判别力，但经Holm校正后统计显著性不足。审计发现5个模型-数据集对存在潜在的基准熟悉度问题（均涉及AsiaM）。

Conclusion: LLM更适合被视为外部验证过的软因果先验来源，而非直接因果结构的可靠证据来源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to provide prior causal knowledge for structural causal discovery, yet whether their direct-edge judgments and confidence can be trusted remains unclear. We systematically evaluate 12 instruction-tuned open-weight models across six benchmark causal graphs, five prompting strategies, and four confidence sources: verbalized, logit-based, cross-prompt agreement, and cross-model agreement. Under our language-only pairwise protocol, our evaluation yields three key findings. (i) LLM-based causal judgments are strongly recall-dominant: models predict overly dense graphs with many false-positive edges, while prompting mainly shifts the precision-recall trade-off rather than resolving overprediction. Gains from model scale diminish on the largest graphs and do not eliminate miscalibration. (ii) LLMs often capture causal relatedness without reliably identifying directness or orientation. Relative to published reference graphs, models misclassify 40.0% of indirect and 36.0% of reversed non-edges as direct edges, versus 28.2% of other non-edges. Moreover, 80.8% and 84.6% of these false positives receive verbalized confidence of at least 80%, revealing substantial overconfidence in structurally incorrect predictions. (iii) Conventional confidence estimates are unreliable, whereas agreement offers a more promising signal. Logit-based confidence frequently collapses near 1.0 regardless of correctness, while cross-prompt and cross-model agreement achieve better mean calibration and discrimination, though their advantages are not statistically significant after Holm correction. A benchmark-familiarity audit further identifies potential familiarity in five model-dataset pairs, all involving AsiaM. Overall, our results suggest LLMs are better viewed as sources of externally validated soft causal priors than as direct evidence of causal structure.

</details>


### [285] [Renormalization Group Flow Matching for Scalable Local Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23696)
*Kanta Masuki,Yuto Ashida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出重正化群流匹配（RGFM），利用重正化群（RG）流作为概率路径，在不同空间尺度上系统地构建数据生成过程。RGFM从长波长结构逐步生成到短波长结构，利用RG的准局域性和尺度分离特性，证明其概率流可由作用范围仅O(Λ⁻¹[ln L+ln(1/ε)])的局域速度场精确近似，从而实现近乎线性的计算复杂度，同时保持长程相关性。实验表明RGFM在1D分布和FFHQ图像上均优于传统局部流匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型面临根本性权衡：全局方法能捕获完整结构连贯性但计算成本高，局部模型高效却难以再现长程相关性和全局连贯性。作者希望弥合这一差距，找到一种既能保持计算可扩展性又能捕获长程结构的生成框架。

Method: 将精确的RG流作为概率路径，构建从长波长到短波长结构的渐进式生成过程。利用RG的两个关键性质——准局域性和尺度分离，证明RGFM概率流可被局域速度场精确近似，近似误差可控，从而可用O(ln L)大小的局部patch进行生成建模，计算成本与系统体积近似线性相关。

Result: 在代表性1D分布上，局部RGFM能再现远超其感受野的长程相关性，而传统局部流匹配在长距离上产生显著误差；在FFHQ图像上，RGFM在64x64和256x256分辨率下生成比局部流匹配更连贯、更高质量的样本。

Conclusion: RG引导的概率流为实现可扩展生成建模提供了一条有前景的路径，仅通过局部计算就能捕获长程结构，有效弥合了全局与局部生成模型之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Despite their remarkable success in modeling complex data, generative models face a fundamental tradeoff. Global approaches can capture full structural coherence but suffer from high computational costs, while local models are efficient but often fail to reproduce long-range correlations and global coherence. The renormalization group (RG) bridges this gap by seamlessly connecting spatial structures across different length scales, retaining quasi-local descriptions at each step while preserving long-range correlations. We introduce renormalization group flow matching (RGFM), a generative framework that systematically structures data generation across different spatial scales. By using an exact RG flow as the probability path, RGFM progressively generates data from long- to short-wavelength structures. To reconcile scalability with global structure, we exploit two key properties of the RG: quasi-locality and scale separation. We rigorously show that the RGFM probability flow can be accurately approximated by local velocity fields acting over a spatial range $O(Λ^{-1}[\ln L+\ln(1/\varepsilon)])$ for RG wavenumber scale $Λ$, linear system size $L$, and prescribed error tolerance $\varepsilon$. This property enables local generative modeling with patches of size $O(\ln L)$ and a computational cost that scales nearly linearly with the system volume. We numerically demonstrate that local RGFM reproduces long-range correlations far beyond its receptive field in representative one-dimensional distributions, while conventional local flow matching exhibits substantial errors at long distances. On FFHQ images, RGFM yields far more coherent and higher-quality samples than local flow matching at 64x64 and 256x256. Our results establish RG-guided probability flows as a promising route toward scalable generative modeling that captures long-range structure using only local computation.

</details>


### [286] [Response Renormalization for Critical Deep Equilibrium Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23725)
*Jose Luis Lima de Jesus Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出响应重整化（Response Renormalization）方法，用于解决深度均衡模型（DEQ）训练中因残差Jacobian近似奇异导致的梯度放大问题。该方法通过在低维临界子空间内选择性地提升近极点分母（CMR和Phi-CMR），在不全局抑制良态敏感性的前提下控制近临界伴随响应。在23个多物理场家族、超过98%静态和95%瞬态实验中，测试误差不超过精确隐式微分训练模型的5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DEQ通过隐式微分训练需要求解基于残差Jacobian的伴随系统。当Jacobian沿损失敏感方向近似奇异时，小扰动会被强烈放大，产生大而高度敏感的梯度，使优化不可靠。现有方法缺乏对近临界伴随放大的选择性控制。

Method: 提出反向传播框架——响应重整化，选择性提升近极点分母，同时保持未提升响应通道不变。包含两种实现：(1) 集体模式响应重整化（CMR），在低维临界子空间应用修正；(2) Phi自适应CMR，基于正磁化率规则计算有界响应质量。还推导了稠密和无矩阵的集体公式，区分了修正冻结锚残差的精确梯度与反向响应代理，并扩展到结构化隐式层与矢量吸引子（SILVA）。

Result: 在23个涵盖偏微分方程、三维场、算子映射、复杂几何和粒子系统的多物理场家族上进行实验。CMR和Phi-CMR在超过98%的静态和95%的瞬态家族-种子比较中，测试误差不超过精确隐式微分训练模型的5%。求解器索引实验显示向静态伴随收敛，物理时间滚动保持预测保真度。

Conclusion: 选择性响应重整化能控制近临界伴随放大而不全局阻尼良态敏感性，使参数更新更可靠，同时保留学习所需的有用梯度信息。该方法为提高DEQ训练的稳定性提供了一种精确且有效的方案。

Abstract: Deep Equilibrium Models (DEQs) compute predictions from a hidden representation unchanged by the model update. Training through this equilibrium uses implicit differentiation and requires solving an adjoint system built from the residual Jacobian. If this Jacobian is nearly singular along loss-sensitive directions, small perturbations can be strongly amplified in the adjoint response, producing large, highly sensitive gradients that can make optimization unreliable. We introduce Response Renormalization, a backward-pass framework that lifts selected near-pole denominators while leaving unlifted response channels unchanged. Collective Mode Response Renormalization (CMR) applies this correction in a low-dimensional critical subspace, while Phi-adaptive CMR computes a bounded response mass from a positive susceptibility rule. We derive dense and matrix-free collective formulations, distinguish exact gradients of a modified frozen-anchor residual from backward-response surrogates, and extend the construction to Structured Implicit Layers and Vector Attractors (SILVA). Across 23 multiphysics families spanning partial differential equations, three-dimensional fields, operator maps, complex geometries, and particle systems, CMR and Phi-CMR yield test errors no more than five percent higher than those from models trained with exact implicit differentiation in more than 98% of static and 95% of transient family-seed comparisons. Solver-index experiments show convergence toward the static adjoint, while physical-time rollouts retain predictive fidelity under the evaluated conditions. These results demonstrate that selective response renormalization can control near-critical adjoint amplification without globally damping well-conditioned sensitivity. Therefore, the method can make parameter updates more reliable while preserving the useful gradient information needed for learning.

</details>


### [287] [Optimal Alternating Regret for Online Learning and Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24731)
*Yixin Tao,Weiqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了在线线性优化（OLO）和在线凸优化（OCO）中minimax最优的交替后悔（alternating regret）问题。对于概率单纯形上的OLO，提出具有O(log d)常数交替后悔的算法并给出匹配下界；对于一般OCO，提出O(d log(1+T/d))的算法并证明匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替后悔是一种由博弈中交替学习动态所驱动的后悔概念，此前在OLO上的最优结果仅为O(log^{2/3}d · T^{1/3})，在OCO上为Õ(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})，尚不清楚是否存在更紧的界限。此外，能否获得O(1/T)收敛到粗相关均衡（CCE）的学习动态是一个悬而未决的问题。

Method: 针对OLO在概率单纯形上的情况，设计了一种能达到O(log d)常数交替后悔的算法；针对一般OCO在d维紧凸集上的情况，设计了具有O(d log(1+T/d))交替后悔的算法。同时为两种情况分别构造了匹配的下界证明。

Result: 1) OLO在单纯形上：O(log d)常数交替后悔，匹配下界；由此获得两玩家零和博弈中O(log d/T)收敛到纳什均衡，两玩家一般和博弈中O(log d/T)收敛到CCE——这是首个具有O(1/T)收敛到CCE的非耦合学习动态。2) OCO在d维紧凸集上：O(d log(1+T/d))交替后悔，匹配Ω(d log(1+T/d))下界，表明Ω(log T)因子不可避免。

Conclusion: 该工作完全确定了OLO和OCO中交替后悔的minimax最优率，显著改进此前所有结果，并为博弈中的学习动态提供了最优收敛保证。

Abstract: We settle the minimax-optimal alternating regret, a regret notion motivated by alternating learning dynamics in games, for both online linear optimization (OLO) and online convex optimization (OCO).
  For OLO over the probability simplex $Δ_d$, we give an algorithm with $O(\log d)$ alternating regret that remains a constant for any time horizon $T$, and a matching lower bound. Our constant regret bound significantly improves previous results with $O(\log ^{2/3}d \cdot T^{1/3})$ regret [Cevher, Cutkosky, Kavis, Piliouras, Skoulakis, Viano, NeurIPS 2023, Hait, Li, Luo, Zhang, COLT 2025]. As a result, we obtain alternating learning dynamics with $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum games and $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to coarse correlated equilibria in two-player general-sum games. This is the first uncoupled learning dynamics with $O(1/T)$ convergence to CCE in two-player general-sum games, while all prior works suffer additional $\log T$ factors.
  For general OCO over a $d$-dimensional compact convex set, we give an algorithm with $O(d\log (1+T/d))$ alternating regret, improving the previous best of $\widetilde{O}(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})$. We also prove a matching lower bound of $Ω(d\log (1+T/d))$, showing that the $Ω(\log T)$ factor is unavoidable.

</details>


### [288] [Calibration-Preserving Pruning: Compression as a Reliability Contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23744)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Adria Binte Habib,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究剪枝模型下的分裂共形预测效率问题，提出校准保持剪枝（CPP）方法，通过非共形梯度显著性增强基础剪枝分数，并采用分离的数据划分策略。实验表明CPP在保持共形覆盖保证的同时，能产生更小的预测集合，尤其在多标签任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分裂共形预测在剪枝模型固定后能提供有限样本的边缘覆盖保证，但单独的效率问题（剪枝能否保留分数几何结构以获得更小的有效预测集）尚未被充分研究。作者旨在解决在保证覆盖的同时减小预测集大小的效率问题。

Method: CPP方法用非共形梯度显著性增强基础剪枝分数，并使用分离的剪枝、验证选择、共形校准和测试四个数据划分。有界分数扰动可推导有界共形分位数偏移和控制集合膨胀，但通用覆盖定理并非CPP特有。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B在50%稀疏度下，DBpedia-14上CPP-SparseGPT将平均集合大小从10.1降至8.6，准确率从0.347提升至0.366；CPP-Wanda从11.2降至9.0，准确率从0.310变为0.295。15个数据集-稀疏度组合中，CPP-SparseGPT在13个中产生更小集合、11个中准确率更高。对照实验表明通用监督梯度贡献了大部分增益。

Conclusion: CPP在可靠性敏感的文本分类任务中能有效减小共形预测集合大小，但真实标签CPP与Wanda+SNIP匹配基线在统计上未显著区分，需阈值感知候选标签CPP以达到更优效果。方法局限性在于仅适用于可靠性敏感的分类任务。

Abstract: Split conformal prediction, not the pruning rule, supplies finite-sample marginal coverage once a pruned model is fixed independently of the conformal calibration split. We study the separate efficiency problem: can pruning preserve score geometry well enough to obtain smaller valid prediction sets? Calibration-Preserving Pruning (CPP) augments a base pruning score with nonconformity-gradient saliency and uses disjoint pruning, validation-selection, conformal-calibration, and test splits. Bounded score perturbations imply bounded conformal-quantile shifts and controlled set inflation, but do not make the generic coverage theorem CPP-specific. Final five-seed Qwen2.5-1.5B results at 50\% sparsity show the largest gains on large-label tasks. On DBpedia-14, CPP-SparseGPT reduces mean set size from \(10.1\) to \(8.6\) while changing accuracy from \(0.347\) to \(0.366\); CPP-Wanda reduces \(11.2\) to \(9.0\) with an accuracy trade-off from \(0.310\) to \(0.295\). Across 15 dataset--sparsity cells, CPP-SparseGPT produces smaller sets in 13 and higher accuracy in 11. Matched controls show that generic supervised gradients explain much of the gain: true-label CPP is not statistically resolved from matched Wanda+SNIP, whereas threshold-aware candidate-label CPP reaches \(7.8\) mean set size at explicit accuracy and offline-compute costs. RoBERTa-base and Llama-3-8B diagnostics support transfer, but our claims remain limited to reliability-sensitive classification.

</details>


### [289] [Tight Majorizations and Convergence Rates of Nuclear Norm Minimization IRLS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23765)
*Christian Kümmerle,Tomas Masak,Dominik Stöger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为约束核范数最小化的迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）方法建立了尖锐的收敛率分析。核心贡献是证明调和平均权重算子定义了全局二次上界（majorizer），并在幂平均权重族中证明其最优性。在Schatten-1零空间性质下证明全局线性收敛，并证明调和平均权重实现维度无关的局部线性收敛率，同时给出反例说明单侧权重算子无法达到该速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IRLS方法是核范数最小化的自然方法，但其收敛速率和权重算子的作用此前缺乏深入理解。现有文献中占主导地位的单侧重加权方案仅利用行或列空间信息，其局限性未被严格刻画。

Method: 1) 提出新的平滑核范数的majorization分析，证明调和平均权重算子构成有效的全局二次上界；2) 在幂平均权重族中证明调和平均权重的最优性；3) 在Schatten-1零空间性质下分析IRLS算法的全局线性收敛；4) 对调和平均IRLS证明维度无关的局部线性收敛率；5) 构造反例说明单侧权重算子的维度相关局限。

Result: 建立了IRLS方法在低秩恢复中的尖锐收敛率；证明了调和平均权重的全局majorization性质及其在幂平均族中的最优性；在零空间性质下证明了全局线性收敛；为调和平均IRLS证明了维度无关的局部线性收敛率；通过反例和数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 调和平均重加权优于传统的单侧重加权方案，能够实现维度无关的更快局部收敛率，并在方形、矩形及对抗性初始化等各类恢复问题中展现实践优势。

Abstract: Iteratively reweighted least squares (IRLS) methods constitute a natural approach to nuclear norm minimization, but their convergence rates and the role of the weight operator have remained poorly understood. This paper establishes sharp convergence rates for IRLS methods for constrained nuclear norm minimization in low-rank recovery. A central ingredient is a new majorization analysis for the smoothed nuclear norm: we prove that the harmonic-mean weight operator defines a valid global quadratic majorizer. Furthermore, we show that this weight operator is optimal within the family of power-mean weights, clarifying why it improves over classical one-sided reweighting schemes that use only row- or column-space information. Under a Schatten-1 null space property, we prove global linear convergence of IRLS algorithms using a variety of weight operators, including the harmonic-mean weights. For IRLS with harmonic-mean weights, we prove a dimension-independent, locally linear convergence rate. We provide a counterexample showing that this dimension-independent local rate cannot in general be obtained for IRLS algorithms using one-sided weight operators, which predominate in the literature. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical results and illustrate the practical advantage of harmonic-mean reweighting across square, rectangular, and adversarially initialized recovery problems.

</details>


### [290] [Single State Update Predictive Coding training for Time Series Forecasting and Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24697)
*Matteo Cardoni,Sam Leroux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新训练技术，将生成式预测编码网络（PCN）与支持编码PCN配对，两者并行训练以匹配神经激活，避免了传统PCN的顺序反向误差传播瓶颈，并将其应用于时间序列异常检测，实现了更稳定、连续、在线的学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码（PC）是一种支持神经网络层并行更新的学习范式，但PCN的主要瓶颈在于顺序的反向误差传播，这限制了其训练效率和应用潜力。

Method: 将生成式PCN与支持编码PCN配对训练，两个网络并行更新以匹配彼此的神经激活，从而消除顺序传播的需求，并应用于时间序列异常检测任务。

Result: 该方法在时间序列异常检测中实现了更稳定、连续、在线的学习效果。

Conclusion: 通过并行配对训练PCN，成功克服了顺序反向传播瓶颈，为预测编码提供了一种更高效的训练方案，并在时间序列异常检测中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Predictive Coding (PC) is a neural learning paradigm that enables parallelizable neural network layer updates. However, the main bottleneck of PC Networks (PCN) is the sequential backwards error propagation. To tackle this, we introduce a training technique that pairs a Generative PCN with a support Encoding PCN. The two PCNs are trained in parallel to match their neural activations, without sequential propagation. We apply this to time series anomaly detection and show that our approach results in more stable, continuous, online learning.

</details>


### [291] [Disentangled Skill Representations for Predictive Human Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23776)
*Mariah Schrum,Deepak Gopinath,Srijan Srivatsa,Guy Rosman,Tiffany Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAIL是一种将人类技能建模为可解释、多维潜在变量的方法，通过持久性技能嵌入和反事实子技能交换实现解耦，在赛车和棒球领域展现出强预测性能和下游AI教练效果的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类技能与典型的潜在变量估计问题不同，它是持久的、组合性的、且基于行为构建的，需要从时间模式中推断，而不仅仅是依赖单一观察。现有的方法难以同时实现技能推断的鲁棒性、可解释性以及跨领域的行为预测泛化能力。

Method: SAIL通过为每个个体学习一个持久的技能嵌入，该嵌入控制专家与新手基座之间的混合，并采用反事实子技能交换进行解缠训练，从而获得既对瞬时表现波动鲁棒又具有结构可解释性的技能表示。

Result: 在赛车和棒球两个领域的实验中，SAIL在预测性能和基于行为的解缠指标上均优于基线方法，同时显著提升了下游AI教练（coaching）的表现。

Conclusion: SAIL证明了对人类技能进行可解释、持久性建模的可行性，其表示既能抵抗瞬时波动，又能支持行为预测和下游人机协作任务，为理解与提升人类技能提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Understanding human skill is important for AI systems that collaborate with, coach, or assist people. Unlike typical latent variable estimation problems which rely on single observations, skill is a persistent, compositional, and behaviorally grounded construct that must be inferred from patterns over time. We introduce Skill Abstraction with Interpretable Latents (SAIL), a method for modeling human skill as an interpretable, multi-dimensional construct inferred from naturalistic behavior. Our approach produces a skill embedding that is robust to transient performance fluctuations and learns a transferable representation of human subskills. Furthermore, SAIL supports skill-informed behavior prediction that generalizes across a variety of in-domain contexts. We represent each individual with a persistent skill embedding that controls a blend between expert and novice bases and is trained using counterfactual subskill swaps for disentanglement. This design encourages representations that are both robust to performance variation and structured for interpretability. We demonstrate across racing and baseball that SAIL achieves strong predictive performance and consistently improves behaviorally grounded disentanglement over the evaluated baselines, while also improving downstream AI coaching performance.

</details>


### [292] [GAP-Prompt: Gated Adaptive Prompting for Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23782)
*Trung-Anh Dang,Duy-Cuong Bui,Ngoc-Son Vu,Christel Vrain,Vincent Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GAP-Prompt（门控自适应提示学习）方法，通过实例级自适应提示学习解决持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，在CIFAR-100、ImageNet-R和CUB-200基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的持续学习方法通常采用静态、任务级别的提示策略，忽略了细粒度的任务内多样性（即同一任务中不同样本的差异），无法实现实例级别的自适应。

Method: GAP-Prompt包含三个协同模块：（1）实例条件门控（instance-conditioned gating），为每个图像动态确定最优的提示注入层；（2）动态知识融合（dynamic knowledge fusion），对当前和历史提示进行实例感知的聚合，实现跨任务知识整合；（3）共享提示蒸馏（shared prompt distillation），在早期共享层锚定基础知识以缓解遗忘。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、ImageNet-R和CUB-200基准上均达到最先进性能。在细粒度CUB-200数据集上达到87.29%的准确率，接近联合训练上界（88.00%），显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GAP-Prompt通过引入实例级自适应机制，有效解决了静态提示策略的局限，在细粒度任务上尤其表现出色，验证了实例级提示选择的优越性。

Abstract: Continual learning faces the persistent challenge of catastrophic forgetting, where sequential task updates degrade previously acquired knowledge. While prompt-based methods integrated with pre-trained models offer a compelling solution by freezing the backbone, they often rely on static, task-level prompting strategies that overlook fine-grained intra-task diversity. In this paper, we propose Gated Adaptive Prompting (GAP-Prompt), a novel method that introduces instance-level adaptability to the prompting process. GAP-Prompt consists of three synergistic modules: (1) instance-conditioned gating, which dynamically determines optimal prompt injection layers for each individual image; (2) dynamic knowledge fusion, which performs instance-aware aggregation of current and historical prompts, enabling knowledge integration across tasks; and (3) shared prompt distillation, which anchors foundational knowledge in early shared layers to mitigate forgetting. Extensive evaluations on CIFAR-100, ImageNet-R, and CUB-200 benchmarks demonstrate that GAP-Prompt consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, on the fine-grained CUB-200 dataset, GAP-Prompt reaches 87.29% accuracy, approaching the joint training upper bound (88.00%) and outperforming existing methods by a significant margin.

</details>


### [293] [Mixture of Channel Experts: Static Sparse Supports with Input-Adaptive Mixing for Pointwise Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23794)
*Elian Iluk,Gil Ben-Artzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoCE（Mixture of Channel Experts），一种受MoE启发的结构化稀疏通道混合层。MoCE将专家轴从算子重复改为通道选择，每个专家是一个输出通道，学习选择k个输入通道的稀疏支持集，并通过预测温度控制softmax在均值与最大值聚合之间切换。该方法替代1x1逐点通道压缩投影，将计算成本从O(C²)降至O(k/C)相对开销，在ImageNet-1K和CIFAR-100等多个任务中匹配或超越密集基线，同时减少16.7%的MACs和端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现直接将MoE设计复制到卷积网络会因结构原因失败：并行卷积专家读取相同输入通道时学习到几乎相同的滤波器。因此需要一种适合CNN的MoE式设计，将稀疏性的利用从算子复制转移到通道选择。

Method: MoCE层包含：(1) 每个专家是单个输出通道，学习k<<C个输入通道的稀疏支持集；(2) 每个输入预测softmax温度，使专家能在均值聚合与最大值聚合之间动态移动；(3) 残差专家汇总未选中的通道；(4) 负载均衡损失保证通道覆盖完整。该层替代1x1通道压缩投影，相对成本随k/C缩放。

Result: 在ResNet骨干网络上的ImageNet-1K和CIFAR-100、迁移学习、EfficientViT以及现代训练配方中，MoCE匹配或超过密集基线和先前的通道选择方法，同时减少16.7%的MACs并降低端到端延迟。预测的计算节省在实测墙钟时间中得到验证。

Conclusion: MoCE证明了MoE理念可以有效地适配卷积网络，关键洞察是专家应该做通道选择而非算子复制。这种方法在保持或提升精度的同时实现了可观的计算和延迟节省。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scales language models by routing each input through a small set of independently parameterized experts. We show that copying this design into convolutional networks fails for a structural reason: parallel convolutional experts that read the same input channels learn nearly identical filters. We therefore move the expert axis from operator duplication to channel selection. We introduce Mixture of Channel Experts (MoCE), a structured sparse channel-mixing layer, inspired by MoE, that replaces pointwise (1x1) channel-reduction projections. In MoCE, an expert is a single output channel with a learned sparse support of k << C input channels. The selected channels are combined by a softmax whose temperature is predicted per input, so each expert can move between mean-like and max-like aggregation. A residual expert summarizes the unselected channels, and a load-balancing loss keeps channel coverage complete. MoCE replaces a dense projection whose cost is quadratic in C with a mechanism whose relative cost scales as k/C, and the predicted savings hold in measured wall-clock time. Across ResNet backbones on ImageNet-1K and CIFAR-100, transfer learning, EfficientViT, and a strong modern training recipe, MoCE matches or exceeds dense baselines and prior channel-selection methods while reducing MACs by 16.7% and end-to-end latency.

</details>


### [294] [A Theory of Speciation in Generative Diffusion Models on Compact Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23798)
*Alessio Marta,Paola Causin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了扩散模型中"物种形成"（speciation）现象的内在理论，基于紧致黎曼流形上的概率密度临界点分岔来刻画物种形成，超越了以往将物种形成等同于对称叉形分岔（pitchfork bifurcation）的大维空间假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论将扩散模型中的物种形成简单描述为对称叉形分岔，并假设工作在高维空间中，缺乏对几何和拓扑作用的严格刻画，无法解释实际观察到的多样化分岔行为和几何依赖性。

Method: 利用谱热核表示显式体现流形几何的作用；运用Poincaré-Hopf和Morse理论对分数平衡点的数量和类型施加全局约束，揭示拓扑强加的几何模态；对热核混合分布证明一般物种形成事件具有一维临界核并承认A2折叠正规形；推导基于几何的物种形成时间估计；建立非退化折叠在分数扰动下的结构稳定性分析。

Result: 证明了非对称配置下的典型物种形成事件表现为A2折叠分岔，而叉形分岔和多方向同时转变仅出现在非对称的对称配置中；给出了双峰混合分布和黎曼正单纯形的几何依赖物种形成时间估计；在球面上用von Mises-Fisher混合分布验证了叉形分岔、鞍结分岔、拓扑模态和层次化多重物种形成；基于图册的神经网络固有分数学习方案在原型和复杂数据集上印证了理论预测的转变。

Conclusion: 物种形成的本质由流形几何和拓扑共同决定，典型的物种形成事件是一维A2折叠分岔而非叉形分岔，且该结构在分数扰动下具有结构稳定性，为理解扩散模型的生成动力学提供了严格的几何-拓扑理论框架。

Abstract: Speciation in generative diffusion models denotes the emergence of distinct stable branches during denoising, through which initially undifferentiated trajectories progressively commit to different data classes. In this work we develop an intrinsic theory of speciation for diffusion models supported on compact Riemannian manifolds: the aim is to go beyond existing theoretical descriptions, which usually identify speciation with a symmetric pitchfork bifurcation and assume to work in a large-dimensional space. We characterize speciation by bifurcations of the critical points of the evolving probability density. A spectral heat-kernel representation makes explicit the role of the manifold geometry, while Poincaré-Hopf and Morse theory impose global constraints on the number and type of score equilibria and reveal topologically-imposed geometrical modes. For mixtures of heat kernels, we prove that generic speciation events have a one-dimensional critical kernel and admit an A2 fold normal form; pitchforks and simultaneous multidirectional transitions arise from nongeneric symmetric configurations. We derive geometry-dependent estimates of speciation times for bimodal mixtures and Riemannian regular simplices. We further establish structural stability of nondegenerate folds under score perturbations and show that the first-order time shift is determined solely by the component of the score error along the critical direction. The theory is illustrated on the sphere using mixtures of von Mises-Fisher distributions, where pitchfork and saddle-node bifurcations, topological modes, and hierarchical multiple speciations are observed. Finally, a chart-based intrinsic score-learning scheme based on neural networks contrasts the theoretically predicted transitions on prototypal and more complex datasets.

</details>


### [295] [Discovering Cross-Language Reasoning Invariance in LLMs with Geometry-Invariant Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23809)
*Igor Bogdanov,Changcheng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过五个人工智能模型在六种语言上解决数学问题，使用CKA和一种新的对比变体稀疏自编码器（GI-SAE）来探究多语言模型是否跨语言共享特征表示，发现特征共享是模型和架构依赖的，且几何相似度并不一定意味着功能可互换性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言语言模型能用不同语言解决相同的数学问题，但不清楚它们是否依赖共享的跨语言特征，还是仅仅产生相似输出的语言特定计算。理解这一机制对多语言模型的内部工作原理和跨语言泛化能力至关重要。

Method: 使用MGSM数据集，在六种语言（英德法西俄中）中保留有效推理轨迹并回放记录多层的表示。先用CKA识别跨语言对齐的层，然后在每层训练两种稀疏自编码器：基线重建模型和新增的GI-SAE（在重建损失基础上加入InfoNCE损失，促使编码器对不同语言相同问题的轨迹产生相似激活）。通过交换特征值并测量KL散度来测试特征的功能可互换性。

Result: GI-SAE在几乎所有层都产生更高的CKA和Jaccard相似度，但更高的几何相似度并不总是意味着更大的功能可互换性。跨语言特征共享是模型和架构依赖的，在不同模型的深度上表现不同：Qwen中增强，Gemma无功能收益，Llama和Phi呈混合的层依赖效应。

Conclusion: GI-SAE主要放大已存在的跨语言结构，而非创造新的共享表示。跨语言特征共享的模式因模型而异，几何对齐指标（如CKA）不能直接预测特征的功能可互换性，未来的跨语言特征研究需要同时考虑几何和功能两个维度。

Abstract: Multilingual language models can solve the same mathematical problem in different languages, but it remains unclear whether they rely on shared features or on language-specific computations that only produce similar outputs. We study this question in five models from four families using the Multilingual Grade School Math (MGSM) dataset, with problems solved in English, German, French, Spanish, Russian, and Chinese, retaining problems with valid reasoning traces in all six languages and replaying those traces through the model to record representations at multiple layers. For each model, we first use Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) to identify layers with cross-language alignment. At each selected layer, we train two sparse autoencoders (SAE): a baseline reconstruction-only model and a contrastive variant introduced in this work, the Geometry-Invariant SAE (GI-SAE). GI-SAE supplements the reconstruction loss with an Information Noise-Contrastive Estimation (InfoNCE) loss that trains the encoder to produce similar activations for traces of the same problem, regardless of language or token position. We then test whether the resulting shared features are functionally interchangeable by swapping their values between languages during the model's forward pass and measuring the resulting change in output, quantified by Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence per feature. Although GI-SAE yields higher CKA and Jaccard similarity at nearly every layer, higher geometric similarity does not consistently imply greater functional interchangeability. We find that cross-language feature sharing is model- and architecture-dependent in this sample and appears at different depths in different models. GI-SAE primarily amplifies cross-language structure already present: the pattern is model-specific, with strengthening in Qwen, no functional benefit in Gemma, and mixed layer-dependent effects in Llama and Phi.

</details>


### [296] [Learning to Grade Efficiently: A Bandit-Driven Prompt-Selection Framework for Low-Cost LLM Essay Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23814)
*Olga Manakina,Igor Bogdanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多臂老虎机（MAB）的自适应提示策略选择框架，用于大型语言模型自动作文评分（AES）。该方法将每种提示类型视为一个臂，在推理过程中动态选择最优评分策略。在雅思写作任务2数据集上，该方法在与穷举网格搜索相当的评分准确度下，将LLM调用次数减少了78.4%。研究还首次生成了作文评分的成本-可靠性学习曲线，为教育技术平台平衡运营成本与评估有效性提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM自动作文评分方法通常使用固定的提示选择策略，无法解决运营成本问题和适应不断变化的最优配置。传统的网格搜索方法成本高昂，且无法在推理过程中动态调整提示策略，因此需要一种能够在保证评分准确度的同时降低成本的在线自适应方法。

Method: 将提示选择问题建模为多臂老虎机（MAB）控制问题，每种提示类型作为一个臂。实现了四种不同的评分方案（多步vs单步评估、有无校准示例），并在雅思写作任务2上进行实验，同时跟踪token使用量、延迟和一致性指标来评估成本-可靠性权衡。

Result: MAB框架在评分准确度上与穷举网格搜索相当，但LLM调用次数减少了78.4%。四种评分方案中，带校准示例的多步评估方案取得了最高的准确度。研究首次生成了作文评分的成本-可靠性学习曲线。

Conclusion: 该工作是首次将在线控制机制应用于AES中的自适应提示策略选择，将提示选择从离线超参数优化问题转化为高效的在线学习任务，为教育技术平台提供了在运营成本和评估有效性之间权衡的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities in automated essay scoring (AES), but contemporary approaches typically employ fixed prompt selection, failing to address operational cost concerns and evolving optimal configurations. We propose a novel cost-aware approach that treats each prompt type as an arm in a multi-armed bandit (MAB) controller, enabling adaptive selection of optimal prompting strategies during inference. Our experiments on IELTS Writing Task 2 essays show that the MAB framework achieves comparable scoring accuracy to exhaustive grid search while reducing LLM calls by 78.4\% to find the best grading approach. We implemented four distinct grading recipes (multi-step vs. single-step assessment, with vs. without calibration examples) and found that the multi-step approach with examples achieves the highest accuracy. By tracking token usage and latency alongside agreement metrics, we produce the first cost-reliability learning curves for essay scoring, providing actionable insights for educational technology platforms that must balance operational costs against assessment validity. This work represents the first application of online control mechanisms to adaptively select prompting strategies in AES, transforming prompt selection from an offline hyperparameter optimization problem into an efficient online learning task.

</details>


### [297] [AQLoRA: A Zero-Search Recipe for Fast Quantized LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23816)
*Md Romyull Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AQLoRA是一种自适应量化LoRA微调方法，通过一次CPU遍历按NF4重建误差对层排序，在内存预算下将Top-K层保留为fp16以避免反量化，从而实现比QLoRA更快的训练速度，同时保持相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLoRA虽然节省内存但训练速度较慢，因为每次前向传播都需要对4-bit权重进行反量化。作者希望在不牺牲精度太多的情况下，找回部分训练时间。

Method: 通过一次CPU遍历（无需搜索或校准数据）对所有层按NF4重建误差排序，在内存预算约束下将Top-K层保留为fp16（跳过反量化）以提升速度。提供两种配置：质量模式（适配所有层）和速度模式（仅适配顶部blocks，使反向传播提前停止）。

Result: 在Commonsense-170K上评估了6个模型、4个架构家族（1.4B-14B）。速度模式比调优的QLoRA快11.1±2.7%（9次独立计时均更快，最差快7%），精度损失约1个点；质量模式快4.8±2.4%，精度与QLoRA持平，比fp16 LoRA差1个点以内，额外内存仅0.2GiB。

Conclusion: AQLoRA有效找回QLoRA丢失的部分训练时间。验证实验发现：按权重密度选择适配层、按量化误差选择保护层均不比随机选择好；保护层的数量（而非身份）是速度提升的关键因素。同时作者总结了在共享硬件上计时的三条经验规则。

Abstract: Quantized fine-tuning (QLoRA) saves memory but not time. It dequantizes every 4-bit weight on the fly, so it trains more slowly than fp16 LoRA. We present AQLoRA (Adaptive-Quantization LoRA), a recipe that buys part of that time back. One CPU pass over the weights sets everything, with no search and no calibration data. The pass ranks layers by NF4 reconstruction error and keeps the top-K in fp16 under a memory budget. Those layers skip dequantization, which is where the speed comes from. A quality setting adapts every layer. A speed setting adapts only the top blocks, so the backward pass stops early. The rule reproduces Unsloth's hand-curated dynamic-4bit selection exactly, in seconds, where search-based allocation needs repeated calibration passes.
  We evaluate on Commonsense-170K across six models and four architecture families, from 1.4B to 14B. The speed setting trains 11.1 +/- 2.7% faster than well-tuned QLoRA and gives up about one accuracy point. It was faster in all nine independent timing sessions, at worst by 7%. The quality setting trains 4.8 +/- 2.4% faster. Its accuracy is level with QLoRA on every model and within a point of fp16 LoRA, for 0.2 GiB more memory. These error bars are measured between independent sessions, not within one. Earning them taught us three rules for timing on shared hardware. Fix the measurement duration, not the step count. Measure the noise floor from a duplicated arm, not a nearly identical method. Repeat whole sessions: a floor computed inside one sweep understates the real uncertainty several times over, and the random seed controls almost none of it.
  We validate the recipe with controls and report the two that failed. Choosing adapter layers by weight density is no better than random. Choosing protected layers by quantization error is not either. The count of protected layers, not their identity, carries the speed effect.

</details>


### [298] [Generating Intervention Hypotheses using Explainable Explanations on Graphs: G2I, a Two-Stage Greedy Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23835)
*Mulin Tian,Ajitesh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将反事实解释重新定义为干预设计问题的方法，通过贪心搜索生成局部反事实并转化为可解释规则，再将网络级干预选择建模为DNF覆盖问题，在预算约束下通过贪心算法求解，实验证明该方法在效率和可操作性上优于基于掩码的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN反事实解释方法（如CF-GNNExplainer和CF²）存在三个主要问题：一是基于连续掩码优化隐含了边可操作的假设，可能将努力分配到不可变或不可操作的属性上；二是计算开销大；三是解释方法本身难以被非AI专家的领域专家理解。作者质疑：简单的启发式方法是否能产生好的解释？

Method: 核心思路是将反事实解释重新框架为干预设计问题。(1) 局部层面：通过贪心搜索直接识别最小化的、可操作的节点特征和邻居条件变化，并推导了贪心搜索有保障的条件，实验验证这些条件近似满足；生成的反事实被转换为可解释规则。(2) 网络层面：将干预选择建模为析取范式（DNF）覆盖问题，在预算约束下，该目标函数是非递减且近似子模的，从而使贪心算法具有理论保证。

Result: 在合成图和真实的自杀风险网络上进行实验，结果表明该方法能够产生可扩展、成本有效的干预策略，相比基于掩码的反事实方法在效率上有显著提升。

Conclusion: 简单方法（贪心搜索+规则转换）可以产生高质量、可操作且可解释的反事实解释，为公共卫生等领域的实际干预决策提供有效支持；DNF覆盖框架为网络级的预算约束干预选择提供了理论基础和高效求解方案。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making in public health and social science can greatly benefit from predictive models, yet translating predictions into effective interventions requires explaining the model behavior. While Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are well-suited for modeling relational data, existing explanation methods largely operate at the node level and fall short of supporting actionable, network-level intervention design. Existing counterfactual GNN explainers, such as CF-GNNExplainer and CF$^2$, rely on continuous mask optimization over features and edges, which implicitly assume feasible edge manipulation, may allocate effort to immutable or non-actionable attributes, and incur substantial computational overhead. Further, the method of arriving at the explanation itself is difficult to explain to a domain specialist who is not an AI expert. Can simple methods generate good explanations? To explore this, we reframe counterfactual explanation as an intervention design problem. At the local level, we generate counterfactuals via a greedy search that directly identifies minimal, actionable changes to node features and neighbor-level conditions. We derive conditions under which the greedy search provides guarantees, and empirically show that these conditions are approximately met. These counterfactuals are converted into interpretable rules suitable for real-world intervention. At the network level, we formulate intervention selection as a Disjunctive Normal Form (DNF) coverage problem under a budget constraint, which is nondecreasing and approximately submodular, enabling a greedy algorithm with theoretical guarantees. Experiments on synthetic graphs and real-world suicide risk networks demonstrate that our approach produces scalable, cost-effective intervention strategies with significantly improved efficiency over mask-based counterfactual methods.

</details>


### [299] [PuzzleKV: Page-Wise Low-Rank Decomposition for KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23843)
*Zizhong Wang,Jieying Wang,Zhao Zhang,Jiajia Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PuzzleKV是一种无需训练和校准的KV缓存压缩方法，将每个head的KV缓存按固定长度划分为逻辑页，将每个完成的页作为独立压缩单元进行低秩分解。在约60%存储下可保留96%以上的Full KV性能，结合量化后仅用18.7%存储即可保留93%以上性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理受限于KV缓存的内存开销。现有低秩压缩方法通常从模型权重或校准激活中构建固定投影空间，或在整个缓存区域构建共享基，这些方法可能无法捕捉细节但重要的信息，因此需要更细粒度的压缩方案。

Method: PuzzleKV将每个per-head KV缓存划分为固定长度的逻辑页，观察到单个页内存在显著的低秩结构。它无需训练和校准，将每个已完成的页作为独立压缩单元，在每层和每个KV head内对页进行分解，直接对稠密页和因子化页计算注意力，并在自回归解码过程中逐步压缩新符合条件的页。

Result: 在匹配存储预算下，PuzzleKV在多个模型、上下文长度和基准上表现有效。约60%存储下达到96%以上的Full KV性能，在RULER上显著优于Global SVD，在LongBench上表现有竞争力；结合量化后仅用18.7%存储仍保持93%以上的Full KV性能。

Conclusion: 将低秩压缩的粒度细化到单个逻辑页级别（而非全局共享基）能更有效地捕捉KV缓存中的重要信息，从而在不牺牲性能的情况下显著降低存储开销，为长上下文推理提供了一种实用的KV缓存压缩方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference in large language models (LLMs) is increasingly limited by the memory required for the key-value (KV) cache. KV cache compression addresses this problem by reducing the storage cost of previous tokens. Among existing approaches, low-rank compression is particularly attractive because it represents every token in reduced dimensions. Previous low-rank methods typically derive fixed projection spaces from model weights, construct fixed spaces from calibration activations, or construct a shared basis over a broad cache region. Such representations may not capture detailed but important information. We partition each per-head KV cache into fixed-length logical pages and observe substantial low-rank structure within individual pages. Based on this observation, we propose PuzzleKV, a training- and calibration-free method that treats each completed page as an independent compression unit. PuzzleKV decomposes pages within each layer and KV head, computes attention directly over dense and factorized pages, and incrementally compresses newly eligible pages during autoregressive decoding.
