The mirror descent framework employs a general Bregman divergence as the distance measure, providing greater flexibility than Euclidean-based methods, while gradient extrapolation exploits historical gradient information to improve convergence performance. Under the restricted strong monotonicity characterized by the Bregman divergence, the convergence of the proposed algorithm is established, and it achieves the $\mathcal{O}(1/k)$ convergence rate with the appropriately selected step-size and parameters. Finally, the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm is verified by an example on the demand response of energy systems.

</details>


### [401] [Optimal Rank Lotteries for Truthful Unit-Interval Covering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24193)
*Alexandros A. Voudouris*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了诚实区间覆盖问题（truthful interval covering），其中每个代理拥有单位长度的私有区间，目标是放置一个公共单位区间以最小化总未覆盖长度，同时激励代理诚实。前人提出了基于订单统计机制（order-statistic mechanisms）的通用策略证明抽签机制，近似比为5/3，并给出了3/2-o(1)的下界。本文填补了这一差距，精确刻画了所有报告无关抽签（report-independent lottery）机制的近似比，证明对每个n≥2，最优近似比为3/2 - 1/(2⌊n/2⌋)。此外，本文还证明了任何通用策略证明随机机制的近似比至少为9/8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补现有文献中关于诚实区间覆盖问题中订单统计抽签机制近似比的上下界差距。前人的工作只给出了5/3的上界和3/2-o(1)的下界，这个间隙需要被精确闭合。同时，需要进一步探索通用策略证明随机机制在更广泛框架下的性能下界。

Method: 本文通过刻画每类报告无关抽签（report-independent lottery over order-statistics）的近似比来闭合此差距。证明了对于任意n≥2，该类机制的最优近似比为3/2 - 1/(2⌊n/2⌋)，该精确刻画是通过数学分析每个可能的抽签分布并求解其最坏情况下的近似比实现的。此外，通过构造对抗性分析，证明了所有通用策略证明随机机制的近似比下界为9/8。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）对每个n≥2，基于订单统计的报告无关抽签机制的最优近似比为3/2 - 1/(2⌊n/2⌋)，从而闭合了之前5/3上界与3/2-o(1)下界之间的差距；（2）证明了任何通用策略证明随机机制的近似比至少为9/8，这给出了一个更广泛类别的下界。

Conclusion: 本文成功精确刻画了基于订单统计的报告无关抽签机制的近似比，闭合了此前存在的理论差距，并提供了对所有通用策略证明随机机制的一个更强的一般性下界9/8。这些结果深化了对诚实区间覆盖问题中随机机制能力的理解。

Abstract: In truthful interval covering, each agent has a private interval of unit length, and the goal is to decide where to place a public unit interval so as to minimize the total uncovered length while incentivizing the agents to be truthful. Previous work proposed a universally strategyproof lottery over order-statistic mechanisms with approximation ratio at most $5/3$, and established a lower bound of $3/2-o(1)$ for this class of mechanisms. We close this gap by characterizing the approximation ratio of every report-independent lottery over order-statistics. In particular, we show that, for every $n\ge2$, the optimal approximation ratio for this class is $\frac32-\frac{1}{2\lfloor n/2\rfloor}$. Beyond rank lotteries, we show that every universally strategyproof randomized mechanism has approximation ratio at least $9/8$.

</details>


### [402] [Transition Systems from Causal Reversible Bundle Event Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24251)
*Nataliya Gribovskaya,Irina Virbitskaite*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了可逆计算背景下，束事件结构（bundle event structures）的两种转换系统语义——基于配置（configuration）和基于残差（residual）——之间的映射关系，证明了这两种语义的同构性，并提供了范畴论层面的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可逆计算是新兴的并行计算范式，而事件结构是并发理论中的基础模型。文献中存在两种结构不同的转换系统语义（基于配置和基于残差），它们各自有不同的应用场景，但二者之间的关系尚未被系统性地澄清，尤其是在带因果可逆性的束事件结构这一具体模型上。

Method: 本文聚焦于带有因果可逆性的束事件结构（用于研究CCS和π演算的可逆扩展），构建了从该可逆事件结构模型到两种转换系统语义（配置型和残差型）的映射，并通过这些映射证明了两种语义之间的同构性，最后给出了映射的范畴论刻画。

Result: 成功建立了可逆束事件结构到两种转换系统语义的映射，证明了配置型与残差型转换系统语义之间的同构关系，并提供了对应的范畴论表征。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上统一了可逆事件结构的两种语义视角，为可逆并发演算的操作语义与指称语义之间的一致性提供了支撑，并借助范畴论提供了更抽象的理论框架。

Abstract: Reversible computing is a novel paradigm that has recently emerged and extends traditional forwards-only computation with the capability to execute in the reverse direction, making it possible for computation to run backwards as well as forwards. Event structures are a foundational model in concurrency theory, providing a way to understand computational processes by describing the events that occur and the relationships between them. In the literature, two structurally different approaches to associating transition system semantics with event structure models have been distinguished. One approach is based on configurations, which are sets of already executed events. The other approach is based on model residuals, which are not yet executed fragments of the model. Configuration-based transition systems appear to be primarily used for semantic representations. Residual-based transition systems are actively applied to demonstrate the consistency between operational and denotational semantics of concurrent process calculi, as well as to visualize the dynamics of models. The present paper focuses on bundle event structures with causal reversibility, which are used in the study of reversible extensions of CCS- and π-like systems. Mappings from the reversible event structure model to the two transition system semantics are developed, which made it possible to prove the isomorphism of the semantics. A category-theoretic characterization of the mappings is provided.

</details>


### [403] [Multilevel Fair Allocation under Additive Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24400)
*Maxime Lucet,Nawal Benabbou,Aurélie Beynier,Nicolas Maudet*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究具有树状层级结构的智能体间的多层级公平资源分配问题，提出了三种基于嫉妒的公平性概念的多层级改编，并分析了多层级加权轮转（MWRR）算法在不同偏好下对这些概念的保证情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公平资源分配研究大多局限于单层级的智能体，然而现实中许多场景（如组织架构、层级行政单位）中智能体之间存在树状层级关系，每个上层智能体的资源需要进一步分配给自己下层的子智能体，因此需要将公平性概念推广到多层级场景。

Method: （1）提出三种基于嫉妒公平性的多层级改编概念（如WEF1的多层级版本）；（2）利用多层级加权轮转（MWRR）算法作为分配机制；（3）理论分析在相同偏好和一般偏好下三种概念的等价性与MWRR的保证情况；（4）通过实验验证MWRR在实际表现中的公平性。

Result: （1）三种多层级嫉妒公平性改编的选择并非中立，它们之间不等价；（2）在智能体偏好相同的情况下，三种改编概念重合，且MWRR能同时保证三者；（3）在一般偏好下，MWRR可能保证某些改编概念但无法保证另一些；（4）实验表明，即使对于MWRR未正式保证的改编概念，其实际表现仍然良好。

Conclusion: 多层级公平资源分配中，公平性概念的具体改编形式会影响分配的理论保证；MWRR是一个实用的分配机制，在多数情形下能提供良好的公平性保证，即使在理论上无法保证某些改编概念时，实验中也表现出较好的性能。

Abstract: We study multilevel fair resource allocation with tree-structured hierarchical relations among agents. At each level, the problem can be viewed locally as allocating an agent's bundle to its children, the overall allocation being a trace of this process iterated down to the leaves. Assuming that internal nodes' utilities are the utilitarian welfare of their children, and the leaves have classical additive utilities over items, we first propose multilevel adaptations of usual envy-based fairness notions (e.g., WEF1). We present three adaptations and show that the choice among them is not neutral. We prove that, under identical preferences, the three adapted envy-based notions coincide, and that the Multilevel extension of Weighted Round Robin (Chakraborty et al., 2021) (MWRR) guarantees them. We then show that under general preferences, MWRR may guarantee some notions while failing others. Finally, through experiments, we show that MWRR may still perform well even for adaptations it does not formally guarantee.

</details>


### [404] [Easier, but Not Easy: Nash Welfare under Lexicographic Valuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24537)
*Soumil Aggarwal,Rohit Vaish,Jatin Yadav*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究字典序遍历估值下不可分割商品的纳什福利最大化问题。主要贡献包括：(1) 提出一个 (1/√2-ε)≈0.707 近似算法，优于一般可加性估值下的 e^{-1/e}≈0.692，并给出匹配的 √2 积分间隙；(2) 针对有序和倍增字典序实例提出精确的多项式时间算法框架；(3) 证明即使有序字典序估值下该问题仍为 NP-hard，且倍增字典序估值下难以获得 0.9996 近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳什福利最大化是资源分配中的核心问题。对于一般可加性估值，已知最佳近似因子约为 0.692 且问题为 APX-hard。作者希望探究字典序估值（大间隔结构使偏好近乎序数化）是否能降低计算难度，从而理解估值结构对问题复杂性的影响。

Method: 论文采用多种技术方法：(1) 使用配置 LP（configuration LP）的舍入算法获得加权纳什福利的 (1/√2-ε) 近似，并证明 √2 的匹配积分间隙；(2) 引入基于支配关系的分支剪枝（branch-and-prune）方法，利用兄弟子树互斥性质，结合矩阵叶子计数论证在常数个智能体下将递归树限制为多项式大小；(3) 通过复杂度归约证明 NP-hard 和近似难度下界。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 加权纳什福利在字典序估值下获得 (1/√2-ε)≈0.707 近似，改进了继承自一般可加性估值的 e^{-1/e}≈0.692；(2) 对有序和倍增字典序实例给出精确的多项式时间算法（智能体数量为常数时）；(3) 证明有序字典序估值下问题仍为 NP-hard，倍增字典序估值下难以获得 0.9996 近似。

Conclusion: 字典序估值使纳什福利最大化"更容易但并非容易"：它允许更强的近似保证和重要情形下的精确算法，但仍保留一般可加性设定中的部分计算困难性，需要精细复杂的技术来处理。

Abstract: Maximizing Nash welfare over indivisible goods is a central problem in resource allocation. For additive valuations, the best-known approximation factor is roughly $e^{-1/e}\approx0.692$, and the problem is APX-hard. We study Nash welfare maximization under lexicographic valuations, where every good is worth more than the total value of all lower-ranked goods. This large-gap structure makes preferences almost ordinal, which might suggest that the problem becomes easy. We show, however, that the picture is more nuanced: although lexicographic valuations enable stronger algorithmic guarantees, they retain significant computational hardness.
  Our first main result is a $(1/\sqrt{2}-ε)\approx(0.707-ε)$-approximation algorithm for weighted Nash welfare under lexicographic valuations, improving over the inherited guarantee of roughly $e^{-1/e}$. The algorithm rounds the configuration LP for Nash welfare, for which we show a matching integrality gap of $\sqrt{2}$.
  Our second main contribution is an exact algorithmic polynomial time framework for ordered lexicographic instances and doubling lexicographic instances. We introduce a domination-based branch-and-prune method for which we prove a mutual-exclusion property between sibling subtrees and use a matrix-based leaf-counting argument to bound the pruned recursion tree by a polynomial when the number of agents is constant.
  Finally, we show that large gaps do not eliminate hardness, as Nash welfare maximization is NP-hard even for ordered lexicographic valuations, and it is NP-hard to obtain a $0.9996$-approximation even for doubling lexicographic valuations. Thus, lexicographic valuations make Nash welfare maximization easier, but not easy: they admit tighter approximation and exact algorithms in important cases, yet still require intricate techniques and preserve some of the hardness of the general additive setting.

</details>


### [405] [Lexicographic Social Ranking on Monotonic Coalitional Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24596)
*Felix Fritz,Stefano Moretti*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究联盟设置中的两种社会排名方法：lex-cel解（忽略联盟规模）和L¹解（通过双字典序比较优先考虑较小联盟）。作者在单调联盟排名框架下，利用最小联盟（关于集合包含关系）的紧凑表示，探索这两种解之间的组合联系，给出了计算参数向量的通用公式和最坏情况运行时间结果，并通过模拟实验评估两种解产生排名的距离，证明单调性假设不会导致排名冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会排名方法在联盟设置中已有多项研究，但lex-cel解和L¹解之间的组合学联系尚未被充分探索。理解这两种解在单调联盟排名（可通过最小联盟紧凑表示）下的关系，有助于揭示它们的行为差异，并为理论与实践提供指导。

Method: 本文采用组合分析方法：在单调联盟排名中利用最小联盟集合作为等价类的紧凑表示；推导lex-cel和L¹参数向量的通用计算公式；分析最坏情况下的运行时间复杂度；并设计模拟实验评估两种解产生排名的距离，验证单调性假设是否导致排名冗余。

Result: 文章给出了从最小联盟计算lex-cel和L¹参数向量的通用公式及最坏情况运行时间结果；通过模拟实验表明，单调性假设并未导致两种解产生的排名出现实际冗余，即两种解在单调排名下仍能产生多样化的排名结果。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了lex-cel与L¹解在单调联盟排名下的组合结构，提供了计算工具和复杂度分析，并通过实验确认单调性约束不会削弱这两种解的排名区分能力，为进一步的理论研究和实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent studies on social rankings in coalitional settings have introduced methods that rank individuals by lexicographically comparing vectors of their occurrences across coalitions ordered according to their strength. In this work, we focus on two such solutions: the lexicographical excellence (lex-cel) solution, which disregards coalition size, and the L^(1) solution, which additionally prioritizes smaller coalitions through a double lexicographic comparison. We investigate the combinatorial connections between these two solutions on monotonic coalitional rankings, where equivalence classes are compactly represented through sets of minimal (with respect to set inclusion) coalitions. After introducing general formulas for computing the lex-cel and L^(1) parameter vectors from these minimal coalitions, we also present worst-case running time results. Finally, to further explore the behavior of the two solutions through simulations designed to assess the distance of the rankings they produce, we show that the monotonicity assumption does not lead to actual redundancy in the rankings produced by the two solutions.

</details>


### [406] [Fair Allocation with Optional Selling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24600)
*Uriel Feige,Yotam Gafni*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究不可分割物品在不同主观估值和市场价格并存下的公平分配问题，定义并分析了EF1/EFX的SEF版本以及MMS/TPS份额概念，得到多组正反结果：两人情形可同时满足MMS和SEFX，三人情形无分配保证每人超过11/12-MMS，任意人数存在2/3-MMS分配及同时满足SEFX和n/(2n-1)-TPS的分配（后者比率最优）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公平分配研究假设物品只能免费分配，但现实中物品往往有市场价格，代理人有主观估值的同时商品可被出售。如何在"决定卖出哪些物品、如何分配未售出物品、如何分配销售收入"这一复杂决策中实现公平，缺少系统研究。作者希望将经典公平性概念（EF1/EFX、MMS/TPS）扩展至带价格销售的场景，并刻画其可行性界限。

Method: 将经典公平概念适配到带市场价格的设置：share-based概念（MMS、TPS）和comparison-based概念（EF1/EFX适配为SEF1/SEFX）。在代理人对物品和货币均具有可加性效用函数的假设下，通过构造性算法获得存在性结果，通过反例构造得到不可能性结果。

Result: (1) 两人情形：存在同时满足MMS和SEFX的分配；(2) 三人情形：存在实例使得任何分配都无法让每人都获得超过11/12-MMS；(3) 任意人数：存在2/3-MMS分配；(4) 任意人数：存在同时满足SEFX和n/(2n-1)-TPS的分配，且该比率在所有情形下是最优的（即使去掉SEFX要求也是如此）。

Conclusion: 研究勾勒出该新设置下公平分配的可能性质边界：两人情形可达到强公平保证，三人及以上出现无法回避的近似损失；同时SEFX与TPS的联合保证比率n/(2n-1)是紧的，表明比较型公平与份额型公平在此设置下存在固有的权衡界限。

Abstract: We consider fair allocation of indivisible goods in a setting in which agents have subjective valuation functions over the set of goods, and in addition, goods may be sold at given market prices. In this setting, a fair allocation involves {deciding which goods to sell, how to allocate the unsold goods, and how to divide the money received from the sold goods.} We adapt to this setting the definitions of share-based fairness notions, such as the maximin share (MMS) and the truncated proportional share (TPS), and comparison-based fairness notions such as EF1 and EFX (which we adapt to SEF1 and SEFX). We show the following results when the utility of each agent is additive both over goods and over money. With two agents, there are allocations that are simultaneously MMS and SEFX. With three agents, there are instances in which no allocation gives every agent more than $\frac{11}{12}$-MMS. With any number of agents, there are $\frac{2}{3}$-MMS allocations. There also are allocations that are simultaneously SEFX and $\frac{n}{2n-1}$-TPS. This latter ratio is best possible, even without the SEFX requirement.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [407] [Integer Natural Evolution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23714)
*Jacob de Nobel,Diederick Vermetten,Hao Wang,Ofer M. Shir,Michael Emmerich,Thomas Bäck*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全基于ℓ₁范数的整数自然进化策略（INES），从第一性原理推导出步长自适应机制。作者证明了双几何（DG）分布属于指数族，其充分统计量|z|可提供色散适应的自然梯度信号，通过进化路径累积该信号得到Ollivier式渐逝记忆在线估计器。实验表明INES在整数二次基准上能学习有意义的逐坐标步长，与整数处理的CMA-ES基线相当，在高维椭球问题和大维度鲁棒收敛方面优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有进化策略处理整数优化问题时，其自适应机制基于ℓ₂高斯模型，不天然适用于整数格。而ℓ₁范数才是Zⁿ上位移的自然度量，双几何分布是其典型变异算子。作者旨在从第一性原理出发，建立完全基于ℓ₁本性的步长自适应机制，而非简单地将连续ES机制离散化。

Method: 作者首先证明双几何（DG）分布属于指数族，其充分统计量|z|产生色散自适应的自然梯度信号。然后通过进化路径累积该信号，按照Ollivier(2018)的框架获得渐逝记忆的在线自然梯度估计器，从而推导出从DG分布的统计结构中直接产生的步长自适应机制，并提出整数自然进化策略（INES）。

Result: 在整数二次基准上的实验表明，INES能学习有意义的逐坐标步长，性能与整数处理CMA-ES基线相当。其优势在高维椭球问题和大维度鲁棒收敛场景中最为显著。

Conclusion: 基于DG分布的步长自适应直接源于变异分布的统计结构，而非连续ES机制的离散类比。INES在整数优化问题上具有竞争力，尤其在维度较高时表现出更好的鲁棒收敛特性。

Abstract: While contemporary Evolution Strategies handle integer optimization problems effectively, their adaptation mechanism is grounded in $\ell_2$-based Gaussian models, which are not native to the integer lattice. In contrast, the $\ell_1$-norm provides the natural measure of displacement on $\mathbb{Z}^n$, with the double geometric distribution as its canonical mutation operator. In this work, we derive a fully $\ell_1$-native step-size adaptation mechanism from first principles and propose an Integer Natural Evolution Strategy. We show that the DG distribution belongs to the exponential family, and that its sufficient statistic $|z|$ yields a natural-gradient signal for dispersion adaptation. By accumulating this signal via an evolution path, we obtain a fading-memory online estimator of the natural gradient, following Ollivier (2018). This establishes that DG-based step-size adaptation arises directly from the statistical structure of the mutation distribution, rather than as a discrete analog of continuous ES mechanisms. Empirical results on integer quadratic benchmarks show that \textsc{INES} learns meaningful coordinate-wise step-sizes and is competitive with integer-handling CMA-ES baselines. Its advantages are most visible in high-dimensional Ellipsoidal problems and in robust convergence at larger dimensions.

</details>


### [408] [Coronavirus Optimization Algorithm: A Success-History Adaptive Evolutionary Framework with Archive-Assisted Search and Stagnation Recovery for Global Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23847)
*Hari Mohan Pandey*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受SARS-CoV-2启发的自适应进化优化算法（COA），用于有界连续全局优化。该算法将冠状病毒机制映射为显式搜索算子（如精英引导吸引、试验向量生成、自适应参数变化等），结合对立初始化、current-to-pbest变异、二项交叉、外部存档等技术，在CEC 2017基准测试（10/30/50维）上对15个优化器进行对比，在所有维度上取得最佳整体Friedman排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索生物启发的优化算法新思路，借鉴SARS-CoV-2病毒机制设计新型自适应进化优化器，而非传统地模拟疾病传播过程。

Method: 将冠状病毒机制映射为显式搜索算子，包括精英引导吸引、试验向量生成、自适应参数变化、停滞恢复和种群规模调度；整合对立初始化、current-to-pbest变异、二项交叉、外部存档、成功历史自适应、种群缩减和部分重启。

Result: 在CEC 2017的29个基准函数（10/30/50维）上，COA在所有维度上取得最佳整体Friedman排名，尤其在组合函数上表现突出；但在部分混合函数上存在局限。

Conclusion: COA是一种简洁、透明且具有竞争力的自适应进化优化器，但仍需进一步的高维验证以评估其扩展性。

Abstract: This paper proposes the Coronavirus Optimization Algorithm (COA), a SARS-CoV-2-inspired success-history adaptive evolutionary optimizer for box-constrained continuous global optimization. COA does not model disease transmission; instead, it maps selected coronavirus mechanisms to explicit search operators, including elite-guided attraction, trial-vector generation, adaptive parameter variation, stagnation recovery, and population-size scheduling. The algorithm combines opposition-based initialization, current-to-pbest mutation, binomial crossover, an external archive, success-history adaptation, population reduction, and partial restart. COA is evaluated on 29 CEC 2017 benchmark functions at 10, 30, and 50 dimensions against 15 competitive optimizers. Results show that COA achieves the best overall Friedman rank across all dimensions, with particularly strong performance on composition functions. The findings demonstrate that COA is a compact, transparent, and competitive adaptive evolutionary optimizer, while also highlighting limitations on some hybrid functions and the need for further high-dimensional validation.

</details>


### [409] [Trust, but Verify: Rigorously Profiling Best-Effort High-Performance Computing for Digital Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23955)
*Matthew Andres Moreno,Santiago Rodriguez Papa,Charles Ofria,Luis Zaman,Emily Dolson*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在HPC（高性能计算）环境下，将"尽力而为"（best-effort）计算范式应用于数字进化领域。研究开发了一个衡量尽力而为代码运行时行为的框架，并通过两个案例验证：CPU集群多进程的多细胞进化模型（64进程下92%扩展效率，2.1倍加速）和Cerebras晶圆级引擎上的模拟（通过稀疏异步采样容忍硬件故障）。作者认为数字进化在推动后确定性HPC范式方面具有独特优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代AI/ML硬件加速平台（如Cerebras WSE）虽提供巨大算力，但受限于设备存储和数据移动，且组件众多易发生故障。传统确定性计算范式难以适应这些约束，需要探索"尽力而为"的宽松计算方法，但这类方法面临可复现性和伪偏差风险挑战。

Method: 开发了一个用于测量尽力而为代码运行时行为的框架，并设计两个数字进化案例研究：(1) 在CPU集群上应用最好尽力而为多进程处理多细胞性进化模型；(2) 在Cerebras WSE上进行通过稀疏、异步设备到主机采样的模拟，以跟踪时空种群历史并容忍硬件故障。

Result: 第一个案例在64进程时实现92%扩展效率（2.1倍加速），即使在硬件异常下仍保持稳健的中位数服务质量。第二个案例展示了在WSE上通过稀疏异步采样策略有效跟踪种群历史并容忍硬件故障。

Conclusion: 数字进化领域凭借其容错性、松散耦合的性质，有独特优势成为探索后确定性HPC范式的先锋；各种形式和规模的"尽力而为"宽松计算值得在该领域进一步深入研究。

Abstract: Developments in high-performance computing (HPC) technology continue to drastically increase quantities of available processing power. In the context of digital evolution, this explosive growth offers opportunities to advance both hypothesis-driven explorations of multi-scale biological phenomena and application-driven evolutionary optimization targeting hard problem domains. A particular opportunity arises from emerging next-generation AI/ML hardware accelerator platforms, such as the 880,000-processor Cerebras Wafer-Scale Engine (WSE). Such hardware, however, constrains on-device data storage and movement --- a challenge compounded by vulnerability to failures arising over numerous device components. Best-effort relaxations that depart from a traditional deterministic computing paradigm can help accommodate such constraints, but complicate reproducibility and risk introducing artifactual biases. We explore these concerns, developing a framework to measure runtime behavior of best-effort code and examining case studies of best-effort computing in digital evolution projects. The first case study applies best-effort CPU-cluster multiprocessing to a multicellularity evolution model, which provides 92% scaling efficiency at 64 processes ($2.1\times$ speedup) and exhibits robust median quality of service, even under hardware anomalies. The second case study examines WSE-based simulations, demonstrating best-effort strategies to track spatiotemporal population history --- through sparse, asynchronous device-to-host sampling that tolerates hardware faults. In sum, across potential forms and scopes of best-effort relaxation, we argue that digital evolution is uniquely positioned to contribute in developing post-deterministic HPC paradigms.

</details>


### [410] [ORBITALIF: An Efficient Spiking Federated Learning Framework for Onboard Cloud Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24073)
*Bohan Zhang,Chenyu Xu,Yijie Mao,Yuanming Shi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出OrbitALIF，一个用于低地球轨道卫星星座云去除的联邦学习框架。它采用仅2.30M参数的脉冲神经网络（SNN）骨干网络，结合自适应门控融合模块（AGFM）和谱空间混合注意力模块（SHAM），通过星间链路实现去中心化联邦学习。实验表明其在云去除质量上具有竞争力，同时在神经形态硬件上每次推理仅消耗0.287mJ能量，相比等效人工神经网络（ANN）降低98.6%能耗（72.3倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统云去除流程需要将含云图像下载到地面站处理，但受限于有限的通信窗口、卫星到地面带宽不足以及高延迟问题。随着LEO卫星星座的规模扩大，需要在轨处理云去除任务以提高时效性和效率。

Method: 1) 构建紧凑的2.30M参数SNN骨干网络，实现低计算开销；2) 设计自适应门控融合模块（AGFM）融合多模态特征；3) 引入谱空间混合注意力模块（SHAM）增强光谱和空间特征提取；4) 采用去中心化联邦学习策略，通过星间链路共享模型权重，实现无需地面站参与的在轨训练和推理。

Result: OrbitALIF在云去除质量上与现有方法相当，但在神经形态硬件上每次推理能耗仅为0.287mJ，较等效ANN实现72.3倍（98.6%）的能耗降低。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了SNN与联邦学习相结合在LEO星座在轨地球观测任务中的可行性，以极低能耗实现接近传统方法的云去除性能，为资源受限的卫星平台提供了一种高效解决方案。

Abstract: Low-earth-orbit (LEO) satellites enable high-resolution, large-scale Earth observation for applications such as disaster monitoring and environmental surveillance. However, cloud coverage often obscures the Earth's surface, and conventional cloud-removal pipelines that download cloudy images to ground stations for processing suffer from limited contact windows, constrained satellite-to-ground bandwidth, and high latency. In this work, we propose a novel satellite federated learning framework for cloud removal across LEO constellations, named orbital attention leaky integrate-and-fire (OrbitALIF). OrbitALIF performs both onboard training and inference using a compact 2.30,M-parameter spiking neural network (SNN) backbone with an adaptive gated fusion module (AGFM) and a spectral-spatial hybrid attention module (SHAM), combined with a decentralized federated learning strategy that shares model weights via inter-satellite links. Our experiments show that OrbitALIF achieves competitive cloud removal quality while consuming only 0.287,mJ per inference on neuromorphic hardware, a 72.3 times (98.6%) energy reduction versus an equivalent artificial neural network (ANN).

</details>


### [411] [On Scaling Coordinate-Based Neuroevolution: The Quadtree Bottleneck in ES-HyperNEAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24480)
*Romain Claret,Michael O'Neill,Paul Cotofrei,Kilian Stoffel*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出JAX-ESHN，一个基于JAX的ES-HyperNEAT GPU并行实现，通过批量CPPN查询和五任务基准测试，揭示其核心结构限制：每个CPPN发现独特的基底位置阻碍vmap向量化。CPU对照实验确认此扩展性差异源于基底发现实现而非GPU硬件。层次空间哈希网格替代方案因丢弃四叉树父门控滤波而失败。这些发现界定了坐标神经进化尺度化必须满足的结构约束，配套EMR-HyperNEAT重构解决了该瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ES-HyperNEAT通过自适应四叉树细分演化基底拓扑，但缺乏全种群级别GPU并行化的实现。作者旨在填补这一空白，并理解为什么难以实现GPU并行化——核心结构问题是每个CPPN发现独特的基底位置集合，防止了通过vmap进行种群级别向量化。

Method: 实现JAX-ESHN（JAX-based ES-HyperNEAT），利用批量CPPN查询进行GPU并行化；在五个任务（XOR、Parity-3、圆分类、正弦回归、CartPole）上与CPU的PUREPLES基线对比；进行CPU对CPU多基准控制实验以排除硬件因素；测试替代数据结构（层次空间哈希网格）并分析其失败原因。

Result: 在XOR任务上，CPU基线运行时间随深度指数增长，而JAX-ESHN的GPU构建成本（编译加第一代评估）在深层基底处趋于平台期。JAX-ESHN在基线罕见成功的场合可靠求解，且运行时方差更低。CPU对CPU多基准控制复现了布尔、连续和控制任务类型的相同扩展性分歧，确认这是基底发现实现的特性。层次空间哈希网格失败是因为它对每个位置独立应用方差测试，丢弃了四叉树父门控滤波及自适应稀疏性。

Conclusion: 这些发现定义了任何基底发现方法若要使坐标神经进化规模化必须满足的结构约束。作为解决方案，EMR-HyperNEAT重新构想——用静态多分辨率网格的急切评估取代自适应细分——满足这些约束并解决了本文表征的瓶颈。

Abstract: ES-HyperNEAT evolves substrate topology through adaptive quadtree subdivision; to our knowledge, no implementation with full population-level GPU parallelization exists. We present JAX-ESHN, a JAX-based implementation targeting GPU parallelization with batched CPPN queries, and benchmark it against the CPU-based PUREPLES Baseline across five tasks: XOR, Parity-3, circle classification, sine regression, and CartPole. The core limitation is structural: each CPPN discovers a unique set of substrate positions, preventing population-level vectorization via vmap. On XOR, the CPU Baseline's runtime scales exponentially with depth while JAX-ESHN's construction cost on GPU (compilation plus first-generation evaluation) plateaus at deep substrates, so JAX-ESHN solves reliably where the Baseline rarely succeeds, with lower runtime variance. A CPU-vs-CPU multi-benchmark control reproduces the same scaling divergence across Boolean, continuous, and control task types, confirming it is a property of the substrate-discovery implementation, not of GPU hardware. An alternative data structure (Hierarchical Spatial Hash Grid) fails not because it precomputes positions but because it applies the variance test independently per position, discarding the quadtree's parent-gated filtering and with it the adaptive sparsity essential to ES-HyperNEAT. These findings define the structural constraints any substrate-discovery method must satisfy to scale coordinate-based neuroevolution; the companion EMR-HyperNEAT reformulation, which replaces adaptive subdivision with eager evaluation of a static multi-resolution grid, satisfies them and resolves the bottleneck this paper characterizes.

</details>


### [412] [Learning Whom to Trust : Decision-Generated Credibility in Social Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24851)
*Gabriel Bontemps,Abhishek Banerjee*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了社会互动中集体学习的张力：社会互动既能改善集体学习，也可能放大早期错误。作者构建了强化学习代理通过漂移-扩散过程做出二元选择的模型，该过程联合决定选择、决策时间和置信度；决策置信度通过加权预期影响和回溯性社会学习成为社会可信度。在均衡社区暴露下，预期场允许精确的商表示，其局部雅可比矩阵是决策敏感性标量乘以社区耦合矩阵，产生了共模放大阈值和跨社区渗透性在阻尼相对差异中的解析作用。蒙特卡洛实验显示非单调性能模式：适度传播加速修正，强传播可能锁定错误共识，低渗透性维持分歧。消融实验揭示置信度的双重角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会互动对集体学习既有促进作用也可能放大早期错误，但当社会信息的可信度是由发送者自身的决策过程产生的（而非预先固定）时，这种张力如何平衡尚不清楚。作者旨在理解决策置信度作为社会可信度来源时，对集体学习动态的影响。

Method: 构建强化学习代理模型，通过漂移-扩散过程做出二元选择，联合决定选择、决策时间和置信度；置信度通过加权预期影响和回溯性社会学习成为社会可信度。利用均衡社区暴露下的精确商表示进行数学分析，并结合蒙特卡洛实验和消融研究验证理论预测。

Result: 发现非单调的性能模式：适度传播加速修正过程，强传播可将群体锁定在错误共识；低渗透性维持分歧。消融揭示置信度的双重角色：可信度敏感的传播放大社会错误，而置信度依赖的私有学习起稳定作用。模型产生了将发送者置信度与接收者行为联系起来的可测试预测。

Conclusion: 社会信息可信度内生生成机制对集体学习具有关键影响，传播强度存在最优区间；跨社区渗透性对维持社区间差异具有阻尼作用；置信度在集体学习中扮演双重角色，既能放大错误也能稳定学习，为理解社会学习动力学提供了新的理论框架和可测试预测。

Abstract: Social interaction can improve collective learning but also amplify early mistakes. We study this tension when the credibility of social information is generated by the sender's own decision process rather than fixed ex ante. Reinforcement-learning agents make binary choices through a drift--diffusion process that jointly determines choice, decision time, and confidence; decision confidence then becomes social credibility by weighting anticipatory influence and retrospective social learning. Under balanced community exposure, the anticipatory field admits an exact quotient representation. Its local Jacobian is a scalar decision-sensitivity term multiplying the community-coupling matrix, which yields a common-mode amplification threshold and an analytical role for cross-community permeability in damping relative community differences. Monte Carlo experiments show the corresponding non-monotone performance pattern: moderate transmission accelerates correction, whereas strong transmission can lock populations into wrong consensus; low permeability instead sustains disagreement. Ablations reveal a dual role for confidence: credibility-sensitive transmission amplifies social error, while confidence-dependent private learning stabilises it. The model yields testable predictions linking sender confidence to receiver behaviour conditional on accuracy.

</details>
